import './App.css';
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { StrudelMirror } from '@strudel/codemirror';
import { evalScope } from '@strudel/core';
import { drawPianoroll } from '@strudel/draw';
import { initAudioOnFirstClick } from '@strudel/webaudio';
import { transpiler } from '@strudel/transpiler';
import { getAudioContext, webaudioOutput, registerSynthSounds } from '@strudel/webaudio';
import { registerSoundfonts } from '@strudel/soundfonts';
import { stranger_tune } from './tunes';
import console_monkey_patch, { getD3Data } from './console-monkey-patch';

let globalEditor = null;

const handleD3Data = (event) => {
    console.log(event.detail);
};

export function SetupButtons() {

    document.getElementById('play').addEventListener('click', () => globalEditor.evaluate());
    document.getElementById('stop').addEventListener('click', () => globalEditor.stop());
    document.getElementById('process').addEventListener('click', () => {
        Proc()
    }
    )
    document.getElementById('process_play').addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (globalEditor != null) {
            Proc()
            globalEditor.evaluate()
        }
    }
    )
}



export function ProcAndPlay() {
    if (globalEditor != null && globalEditor.repl.state.started == true) {
        console.log(globalEditor)
        Proc()
        globalEditor.evaluate();
    }
}

export function Proc() {

    let proc_text = document.getElementById('proc').value
    let proc_text_replaced = proc_text.replaceAll('<p1_Radio>', ProcessText);
    ProcessText(proc_text);
    globalEditor.setCode(proc_text_replaced)
}

export function ProcessText(match, ...args) {

    let replace = ""
    if (document.getElementById('flexRadioDefault2').checked) {
        replace = "_"
    }

    return replace
}

export default function StrudelDemo() {

const hasRun = useRef(false);

useEffect(() => {

    if (!hasRun.current) {
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        console_monkey_patch();
        hasRun.current = true;
        //Code copied from example: https://codeberg.org/uzu/strudel/src/branch/main/examples/codemirror-repl
            //init canvas
            const canvas = document.getElementById('roll');
            canvas.width = canvas.width * 2;
            canvas.height = canvas.height * 2;
            const drawContext = canvas.getContext('2d');
            const drawTime = [-2, 2]; // time window of drawn haps
            globalEditor = new StrudelMirror({
                defaultOutput: webaudioOutput,
                getTime: () => getAudioContext().currentTime,
                transpiler,
                root: document.getElementById('editor'),
                drawTime,
                onDraw: (haps, time) => drawPianoroll({ haps, time, ctx: drawContext, drawTime, fold: 0 }),
                prebake: async () => {
                    initAudioOnFirstClick(); // needed to make the browser happy (don't await this here..)
                    const loadModules = evalScope(
                        import('@strudel/core'),
                        import('@strudel/draw'),
                        import('@strudel/mini'),
                        import('@strudel/tonal'),
                        import('@strudel/webaudio'),
                    );
                    await Promise.all([loadModules, registerSynthSounds(), registerSoundfonts()]);
                },
            });
            
        document.getElementById('proc').value = stranger_tune
        SetupButtons()
        Proc()
    }

}, []);


return (
    <div>
        <h2>Strudel Demo</h2>
        <main>

            <div className="container-fluid">
                <div className="row">
                    <div className="col-md-8" style={{ maxHeight: '50vh', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                        <label htmlFor="exampleFormControlTextarea1" className="form-label">Text to preprocess:</label>
                        <textarea className="form-control" rows="15" id="proc" ></textarea>
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-md-4">

                        <nav>
                            <button id="process" className="btn btn-outline-primary">Preprocess</button>
                            <button id="process_play" className="btn btn-outline-primary">Proc & Play</button>
                            <br />
                            <button id="play" className="btn btn-outline-primary">Play</button>
                            <button id="stop" className="btn btn-outline-primary">Stop</button>
                        </nav>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div className="row">
                    <div className="col-md-8" style={{ maxHeight: '50vh', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                        <div id="editor" />
                        <div id="output" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-md-4">
                        <div className="form-check">
                            <input className="form-check-input" type="radio" name="flexRadioDefault" id="flexRadioDefault1" onChange={ProcAndPlay} defaultChecked />
                            <label className="form-check-label" htmlFor="flexRadioDefault1">
                                p1: ON
                            </label>
                        </div>
                        <div className="form-check">
                            <input className="form-check-input" type="radio" name="flexRadioDefault" id="flexRadioDefault2" onChange={ProcAndPlay} />
                            <label className="form-check-label" htmlFor="flexRadioDefault2">
                                p1: HUSH
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <canvas id="roll"></canvas>
        </main >
    </div >
);


}
ChatGPT said:

Got it 👍 — you want your current vanilla React component code restructured into a cleaner React-style framework (so no direct document.getElementById event binding or side effects outside React’s lifecycle).

Here’s a refactored version that preserves the same logic but makes it idiomatic React — using React refs, state, and event handlers instead of DOM lookups.

✅ Restructured React Version (StrudelDemo.jsx)
import './App.css';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { StrudelMirror } from '@strudel/codemirror';
import { evalScope } from '@strudel/core';
import { drawPianoroll } from '@strudel/draw';
import { initAudioOnFirstClick } from '@strudel/webaudio';
import { transpiler } from '@strudel/transpiler';
import { getAudioContext, webaudioOutput, registerSynthSounds } from '@strudel/webaudio';
import { registerSoundfonts } from '@strudel/soundfonts';
import { stranger_tune } from './tunes';
import console_monkey_patch, { getD3Data } from './console-monkey-patch';

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [radioValue, setRadioValue] = useState('on');
  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);

  // Handle external D3 events
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
    document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    console_monkey_patch();
    return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
  }, []);

  // Initialize StrudelMirror
  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasRun.current) return;
    hasRun.current = true;

    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width *= 2;
    canvas.height *= 2;

    const drawTime = [-2, 2];

    const globalEditor = new StrudelMirror({
      defaultOutput: webaudioOutput,
      getTime: () => getAudioContext().currentTime,
      transpiler,
      root: editorContainerRef.current,
      drawTime,
      onDraw: (haps, time) => drawPianoroll({ haps, time, ctx, drawTime, fold: 0 }),
      prebake: async () => {
        initAudioOnFirstClick();
        const loadModules = evalScope(
          import('@strudel/core'),
          import('@strudel/draw'),
          import('@strudel/mini'),
          import('@strudel/tonal'),
          import('@strudel/webaudio'),
        );
        await Promise.all([loadModules, registerSynthSounds(), registerSoundfonts()]);
      },
    });

    globalEditorRef.current = globalEditor;
    globalEditor.setCode(processText(procText, radioValue));
  }, [procText, radioValue]);

  // --- Logic functions ---
  const processText = (text, mode) => {
    const replace = mode === 'hush' ? '_' : '';
    return text.replaceAll('<p1_Radio>', replace);
  };

  const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText, radioValue);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
  };

  const handlePlay = () => {
    globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
  };

  const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
    if (globalEditorRef.current?.repl?.state?.started) {
      handleProcess();
      handlePlay();
    }
  };

  const handleStop = () => {
    globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
  };

  // --- UI ---
  return (
    <div>
      <h2>Strudel Demo</h2>
      <main>
        <div className="container-fluid">
          <div className="row">
            {/* Left: Text area */}
            <div className="col-md-8" style={{ maxHeight: '50vh', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
              <label htmlFor="proc" className="form-label">
                Text to preprocess:
              </label>
              <textarea
                id="proc"
                className="form-control"
                rows="15"
                value={procText}
                onChange={(e) => setProcText(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>

            {/* Right: Buttons */}
            <div className="col-md-4">
              <nav>
                <button onClick={handleProcess} className="btn btn-outline-primary">Preprocess</button>
                <button onClick={handleProcAndPlay} className="btn btn-outline-primary">Proc & Play</button>
                <br />
                <button onClick={handlePlay} className="btn btn-outline-primary">Play</button>
                <button onClick={handleStop} className="btn btn-outline-primary">Stop</button>
              </nav>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Editor + Controls */}
          <div className="row">
            <div className="col-md-8" style={{ maxHeight: '50vh', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
              <div ref={editorContainerRef} id="editor" />
              <div id="output" />
            </div>

            <div className="col-md-4">
              <div className="form-check">
                <input
                  className="form-check-input"
                  type="radio"
                  name="p1"
                  id="p1_on"
                  value="on"
                  checked={radioValue === 'on'}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    setRadioValue(e.target.value);
                    handleProcAndPlay();
                  }}
                />
                <label className="form-check-label" htmlFor="p1_on">
                  p1: ON
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="form-check">
                <input
                  className="form-check-input"
                  type="radio"
                  name="p1"
                  id="p1_hush"
                  value="hush"
                  checked={radioValue === 'hush'}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    setRadioValue(e.target.value);
                    handleProcAndPlay();
                  }}
                />
                <label className="form-check-label" htmlFor="p1_hush">
                  p1: HUSH
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <canvas ref={canvasRef} id="roll"></canvas>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

🔧 Improvements Made

✅ Removed all document.getElementById and direct event bindings
→ Replaced with React ref and onClick handlers.

✅ React-managed state for textarea & radio buttons
→ Enables reactivity and re-rendering automatically.

✅ Global editor handled via a ref (globalEditorRef)
→ Prevents reinitialization and safely references StrudelMirror instance.

✅ Lifecycle-safe initialization
→ The useEffect ensures the editor and audio only initialize once.

✅ Cleaner modular function design
→ Each button now calls its logical equivalent without touching DOM manually.

Would you like me to split the code into separate React components (e.g. Controls, EditorPane, CanvasView) for better modularity and maintainability?

You said:

ChatGPT said:

Perfect — splitting the React app into smaller files will make it much cleaner and easier to maintain.
Here’s how you can break your StrudelDemo app into organized, modular files.

📁 Folder structure
src/
│
├── components/
│   ├── StrudelDemo.jsx
│   ├── EditorPane.jsx
│   ├── Controls.jsx
│   ├── RadioGroup.jsx
│   └── CanvasView.jsx
│
├── utils/
│   ├── strudelSetup.js
│   ├── textProcessor.js
│   └── console-monkey-patch.js
│
├── tunes.js
└── App.jsx

🧩 components/StrudelDemo.jsx

Main container component — brings everything together.

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";

import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import RadioGroup from "./RadioGroup";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [radioValue, setRadioValue] = useState("on");
  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    console_monkey_patch();

    const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
    document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);

    return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasRun.current) return;
    hasRun.current = true;

    globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
      editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
      canvas: canvasRef.current,
      procText,
      radioValue,
    });
  }, []);

  const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText, radioValue);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
  };

  const handlePlay = () => {
    globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
  };

  const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
    if (globalEditorRef.current?.repl?.state?.started) {
      handleProcess();
      handlePlay();
    }
  };

  const handleStop = () => {
    globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <h2>Strudel Demo</h2>
      <main className="container-fluid">
        <div className="row">
          <EditorPane value={procText} onChange={setProcText} />
          <Controls
            onProcess={handleProcess}
            onProcPlay={handleProcAndPlay}
            onPlay={handlePlay}
            onStop={handleStop}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="row">
          <div className="col-md-8" style={{ maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" }}>
            <div ref={editorContainerRef} id="editor" />
            <div id="output" />
          </div>
          <RadioGroup
            radioValue={radioValue}
            onChange={(val) => {
              setRadioValue(val);
              handleProcAndPlay();
            }}
          />
        </div>

        <CanvasView ref={canvasRef} />
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

🧩 components/EditorPane.jsx

      <div className="form-check">
        <input
          className="form-check-input"
          type="radio"
          name="p1"
          id="p1_on"
          value="on"
          checked={radioValue === "on"}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        />
        <label className="form-check-label" htmlFor="p1_on">
          p1: ON
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="form-check">
        <input
          className="form-check-input"
          type="radio"
          name="p1"
          id="p1_hush"
          value="hush"
          checked={radioValue === "hush"}

🧩 components/CanvasView.jsx
import { forwardRef } from "react";

const CanvasView = forwardRef((_, ref) => (
  <canvas ref={ref} id="roll" />
));

export default CanvasView;

🧠 utils/textProcessor.js
export function processText(text, mode) {
  const replace = mode === "hush" ? "_" : "";
  return text.replaceAll("<p1_Radio>", replace);
}

⚙️ utils/strudelSetup.js
import { StrudelMirror } from "@strudel/codemirror";
import { evalScope } from "@strudel/core";
import { drawPianoroll } from "@strudel/draw";
import { initAudioOnFirstClick, webaudioOutput, getAudioContext, registerSynthSounds } from "@strudel/webaudio";
import { registerSoundfonts } from "@strudel/soundfonts";
import { transpiler } from "@strudel/transpiler";
import { processText } from "./textProcessor";

export function initStrudelEditor({ editorContainer, canvas, procText, radioValue }) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  canvas.width *= 2;
  canvas.height *= 2;
  const drawTime = [-2, 2];

  const editor = new StrudelMirror({
    defaultOutput: webaudioOutput,
    getTime: () => getAudioContext().currentTime,
    transpiler,
    root: editorContainer,
    drawTime,
    onDraw: (haps, time) => drawPianoroll({ haps, time, ctx, drawTime, fold: 0 }),
    prebake: async () => {
      initAudioOnFirstClick();
      const loadModules = evalScope(
        import("@strudel/core"),
        import("@strudel/draw"),
        import("@strudel/mini"),
        import("@strudel/tonal"),
        import("@strudel/webaudio")
      );
      await Promise.all([loadModules, registerSynthSounds(), registerSoundfonts()]);
    },
  });

  const initialCode = processText(procText, radioValue);
  editor.setCode(initialCode);

  return editor;
}

🧩 App.jsx
import './App.css';
import StrudelDemo from './components/StrudelDemo';

function App() {
  return (
    <div className="App">
      <StrudelDemo />
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

✅ Benefits of this structure

Clear separation of UI & logic

React components only handle rendering.

Utility files handle setup & processing.

Easier maintenance

You can edit or replace one module without touching others.

Reusability

Components like Controls or RadioGroup can be reused elsewhere.

Cleaner state flow

Everything controlled by React hooks — no more document.getElementById hacks.

🧩 components/StrudelDemo.js
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";

import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import RadioGroup from "./RadioGroup";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [radioValue, setRadioValue] = useState("on");
  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    console_monkey_patch();
    const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
    document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasRun.current) return;
    hasRun.current = true;
    globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
      editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
      canvas: canvasRef.current,
      procText,
      radioValue,
    });
  }, []);

  const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText, radioValue);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
  };

  const handlePlay = () => globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();

  const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
    if (globalEditorRef.current?.repl?.state?.started) {
      handleProcess();
      handlePlay();
    }
  };

  const handleStop = () => globalEditorRef.current?.stop();

  return React.createElement(
    "div",
    null,
    React.createElement("h2", null, "Strudel Demo"),
    React.createElement(
      "main",
      { className: "container-fluid" },
      React.createElement(
        "div",
        { className: "row" },
        React.createElement(EditorPane, { value: procText, onChange: setProcText }),
        React.createElement(Controls, {
          onProcess: handleProcess,
          onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
          onPlay: handlePlay,
          onStop: handleStop,
        })
      ),
      React.createElement(
        "div",
        { className: "row" },
        React.createElement(
          "div",
          { className: "col-md-8", style: { maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" } },
          React.createElement("div", { ref: editorContainerRef, id: "editor" }),
          React.createElement("div", { id: "output" })
        ),
        React.createElement(RadioGroup, {
          radioValue: radioValue,
          onChange: (val) => {
            setRadioValue(val);
            handleProcAndPlay();
          },
        })
      ),
      React.createElement(CanvasView, { ref: canvasRef })
    )
  );
}

🧩 components/EditorPane.js
import React from "react";

export default function EditorPane({ value, onChange }) {
  return React.createElement(
    "div",
    { className: "col-md-8", style: { maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" } },
    React.createElement("label", { htmlFor: "proc", className: "form-label" }, "Text to preprocess:"),
    React.createElement("textarea", {
      id: "proc",
      className: "form-control",
      rows: 15,
      value,
      onChange: (e) => onChange(e.target.value),
    })
  );
}

🧩 components/Controls.js
import React from "react";

export default function Controls({ onProcess, onProcPlay, onPlay, onStop }) {
  return React.createElement(
    "div",
    { className: "col-md-4" },
    React.createElement(
      "nav",
      null,
      React.createElement("button", { onClick: onProcess, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Preprocess"),
      React.createElement("button", { onClick: onProcPlay, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Proc & Play"),
      React.createElement("br"),
      React.createElement("button", { onClick: onPlay, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Play"),
      React.createElement("button", { onClick: onStop, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Stop")
    )
  );
}

🧩 components/RadioGroup.js
import React from "react";

export default function RadioGroup({ radioValue, onChange }) {
  return React.createElement(
    "div",
    { className: "col-md-4" },
    React.createElement(
      "div",
      { className: "form-check" },
      React.createElement("input", {
        className: "form-check-input",
        type: "radio",
        name: "p1",
        id: "p1_on",
        value: "on",
        checked: radioValue === "on",
        onChange: (e) => onChange(e.target.value),
      }),
      React.createElement("label", { className: "form-check-label", htmlFor: "p1_on" }, "p1: ON")
    ),
    React.createElement(
      "div",
      { className: "form-check" },
      React.createElement("input", {
        className: "form-check-input",
        type: "radio",
        name: "p1",
        id: "p1_hush",
        value: "hush",
        checked: radioValue === "hush",
        onChange: (e) => onChange(e.target.value),
      }),
      React.createElement("label", { className: "form-check-label", htmlFor: "p1_hush" }, "p1: HUSH")
    )
  );
}

🧩 components/CanvasView.js
import React, { forwardRef } from "react";

const CanvasView = forwardRef(function CanvasView(_, ref) {
  return React.createElement("canvas", { ref, id: "roll" });
});

export default CanvasView;

🧠 utils/textProcessor.js
export function processText(text, mode) {
  const replace = mode === "hush" ? "_" : "";
  return text.replaceAll("<p1_Radio>", replace);
}

⚙️ utils/strudelSetup.js
import { StrudelMirror } from "@strudel/codemirror";
import { evalScope } from "@strudel/core";
import { drawPianoroll } from "@strudel/draw";
import {
  initAudioOnFirstClick,
  webaudioOutput,
  getAudioContext,
  registerSynthSounds,
} from "@strudel/webaudio";
import { registerSoundfonts } from "@strudel/soundfonts";
import { transpiler } from "@strudel/transpiler";
import { processText } from "./textProcessor";

export function initStrudelEditor({ editorContainer, canvas, procText, radioValue }) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  canvas.width *= 2;
  canvas.height *= 2;
  const drawTime = [-2, 2];

  const editor = new StrudelMirror({
    defaultOutput: webaudioOutput,
    getTime: () => getAudioContext().currentTime,
    transpiler,
    root: editorContainer,
    drawTime,
    onDraw: (haps, time) => drawPianoroll({ haps, time, ctx, drawTime, fold: 0 }),
    prebake: async () => {
      initAudioOnFirstClick();
      const loadModules = evalScope(
        import("@strudel/core"),
        import("@strudel/draw"),
        import("@strudel/mini"),
        import("@strudel/tonal"),
        import("@strudel/webaudio")
      );
      await Promise.all([loadModules, registerSynthSounds(), registerSoundfonts()]);
    },
  });

  editor.setCode(processText(procText, radioValue));
  return editor;
}

🔍 The error:
Uncaught TypeError: _utils_console_monkey_patch__WEBPACK_IMPORTED_MODULE_4___default(...) is not a function


means this line inside your StrudelDemo.js:

console_monkey_patch();


is trying to call something that isn’t actually a function.

So React imported console_monkey_patch incorrectly, or your console-monkey-patch.js file doesn’t export a function the way the import expects.

🧩 The problem

Your file likely looks something like this:

export const getD3Data = () => { ... }
export default { getD3Data }


or maybe

export function console_monkey_patch() { ... }


but you’re importing it like this:

import console_monkey_patch, { getD3Data } from '../utils/console-monkey-patch';


If the default export isn’t actually a function, then calling it (console_monkey_patch()) throws the error.

✅ The fix (pick one option below)
🧠 Option 1 – if your file has a named export only

If console-monkey-patch.js looks like:

export function console_monkey_patch() { ... }
export function getD3Data() { ... }


then import it correctly:

import { console_monkey_patch, getD3Data } from '../utils/console-monkey-patch';

🧠 Option 2 – if your file uses default export

If console-monkey-patch.js has:

export default function console_monkey_patch() { ... }
export function getD3Data() { ... }


then your import should be:

import console_monkey_patch, { getD3Data } from '../utils/console-monkey-patch';


…but make sure the default really is a function (not an object or undefined).

🧰 Quick way to verify

Inside StrudelDemo.js, before calling it, add:

console.log('console_monkey_patch =', console_monkey_patch);

ƒ console_monkey_patch() → ✅ fine

{ getD3Data: f } or {} → ❌ you’re importing it wrong

ChatGPT said:

import console_monkey_patch, { getD3Data } from '../utils/console-monkey-patch';

✅ src/utils/console-monkey-patch.js
// console-monkey-patch.js
// This patches console.log to dispatch a "d3Data" event when certain data is logged.
// It also exports a helper for retrieving the last dispatched data.

let lastD3Data = null;

export default function console_monkey_patch() {
  const originalLog = console.log;

  console.log = function (...args) {
    // Forward all logs normally
    originalLog.apply(console, args);

    // If a special D3-like data object is detected, dispatch it
    const d3Candidate = args.find(
      (arg) => arg && typeof arg === 'object' && arg.type === 'd3Data'
    );

    if (d3Candidate) {
      lastD3Data = d3Candidate;
      const event = new CustomEvent('d3Data', { detail: d3Candidate });
      window.dispatchEvent(event);
    }
  };
}

export function getD3Data() {
  return lastD3Data;
}

✅ Why this works

The default export is a callable function (console_monkey_patch()), so your existing line

console_monkey_patch();


will no longer crash.

getD3Data remains a named export.

Event dispatching (window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(...))) matches what your React component listens for.

After saving, restart your dev server (npm start or yarn start) —
your StrudelDemo should now load and render normally.

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import '../utils/console-monkey-patch';
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import RadioGroup from "./RadioGroup";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [radioValue, setRadioValue] = useState("on");
    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
            radioValue,
        });
    }, []);

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText, radioValue);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handlePlay = () => globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        if (globalEditorRef.current?.repl?.state?.started) {
            handleProcess();
            handlePlay();
        }
    };

    const handleStop = () => globalEditorRef.current?.stop();

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        null,
        React.createElement("h2", null, "Strudel Demo"),
        React.createElement(
            "main",
            { className: "container-fluid" },
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(EditorPane, { value: procText, onChange: setProcText }),
                React.createElement(Controls, {
                    onProcess: handleProcess,
                    onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                    onPlay: handlePlay,
                    onStop: handleStop,
                })
            ),
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-md-8", style: { maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" } },
                    React.createElement("div", { ref: editorContainerRef, id: "editor" }),
                    React.createElement("div", { id: "output" })
                ),
                React.createElement(RadioGroup, {
                    radioValue: radioValue,
                    onChange: (val) => {
                        setRadioValue(val);
                        handleProcAndPlay();
                    },
                })
            ),
            React.createElement(CanvasView, { ref: canvasRef })
        )
    );
}

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import RadioGroup from "./RadioGroup";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";


export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [radioValue, setRadioValue] = useState("on");
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140); // initial BPM
    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
            radioValue,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update setcps line in tune when BPM changes
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(
            /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
            setcps(${bpm}/60/4)
        );
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText, radioValue);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handlePlay = () => globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        if (globalEditorRef.current?.repl?.state?.started) {
            handleProcess();
            handlePlay();
        }
    };

    const handleStop = () => globalEditorRef.current?.stop();

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        null,
        React.createElement("h2", null, "Strudel Demo"),
        React.createElement(
            "main",
            { className: "container-fluid" },
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(EditorPane, { value: procText, onChange: setProcText }),
                React.createElement(Controls, {
                    onProcess: handleProcess,
                    onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                    onPlay: handlePlay,
                    onStop: handleStop,
                })
            ),

            // BPM Slider
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row my-3 align-items-center" },
                React.createElement("div", { className: "col-md-8" },
                    React.createElement("label", { htmlFor: "bpmSlider" }, BPM: ${bpm}),
                    React.createElement("input", {
                        id: "bpmSlider",
                        type: "range",
                        min: "60",
                        max: "200",
                        value: bpm,
                        onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                        className: "form-range w-100"
                    })
                )
            ),

            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-md-8", style: { maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" } },
                    React.createElement("div", { ref: editorContainerRef, id: "editor" }),
                    React.createElement("div", { id: "output" })
                ),
                React.createElement(RadioGroup, {
                    radioValue: radioValue,
                    onChange: (val) => {
                        setRadioValue(val);
                        handleProcAndPlay();
                    },
                })
            ),
            React.createElement(CanvasView, { ref: canvasRef })
        )
    );
}

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import RadioGroup from "./RadioGroup";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [radioValue, setRadioValue] = useState("on");
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
    const [bpmTimeline, setBpmTimeline] = useState([]);

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null); // store interval so we can clear it

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
            radioValue,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update Strudel code when BPM changes
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(
            /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
            setcps(${bpm}/60/4)
        );
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);

        // Record BPM if playing
        if (playing) {
            setBpmTimeline(prev => [...prev, { time: currentTime, bpm }]);
        }
    }, [bpm]);

    // Manage playback interval
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return; // already running
        setPlaying(true);
        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            setCurrentTime(t => {
                const newTime = t + 0.1;
                // record BPM every tick
                setBpmTimeline(prev => [...prev, { time: newTime, bpm }]);
                return newTime;
            });
        }, 100);
        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText, radioValue);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        if (globalEditorRef.current?.repl?.state?.started) {
            handleProcess();
            startPlayback();
        }
    };

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        null,
        React.createElement("h2", null, "Strudel Demo"),
        React.createElement(
            "main",
            { className: "container-fluid" },
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(EditorPane, { value: procText, onChange: setProcText }),
                React.createElement(Controls, {
                    onProcess: handleProcess,
                    onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                    onPlay: startPlayback,
                    onStop: stopPlayback,
                })
            ),

            // BPM Slider + Line Graph
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row my-3 align-items-center" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-md-8" },
                    React.createElement("label", { htmlFor: "bpmSlider" }, BPM: ${bpm}),
                    React.createElement("input", {
                        id: "bpmSlider",
                        type: "range",
                        min: "60",
                        max: "200",
                        value: bpm,
                        onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                        className: "form-range w-100"
                    }),
                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                        bpmData: bpmTimeline,
                        currentTime,
                        width: 600,
                        height: 100,
                        playing
                    })
                )
            ),

            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-md-8", style: { maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" } },
                    React.createElement("div", { ref: editorContainerRef, id: "editor" }),
                    React.createElement("div", { id: "output" })
                ),
                React.createElement(RadioGroup, {
                    radioValue: radioValue,
                    onChange: (val) => {
                        setRadioValue(val);
                        handleProcAndPlay();
                    },
                })
            ),
            React.createElement(CanvasView, { ref: canvasRef })
        )
    );
}


import React, { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import * as d3 from "d3";

export default function BPMGraph({ bpmTimeline = [], currentTime = 0, width = 800, height = 150, playing }) {
    const svgRef = useRef();

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!playing || !bpmTimeline.length) return; // protect from empty/undefined

        const svg = d3.select(svgRef.current);
        svg.selectAll("*").remove();

        const margin = { top: 10, right: 20, bottom: 20, left: 40 };
        const innerWidth = width - margin.left - margin.right;
        const innerHeight = height - margin.top - margin.bottom;

        const g = svg.append("g").attr("transform", translate(${margin.left},${margin.top}));

        const timeWindow = 5;
        const visibleData = bpmTimeline.filter(d => d.time >= currentTime - timeWindow);

        const xScale = d3.scaleLinear()
            .domain([currentTime - timeWindow, currentTime])
            .range([0, innerWidth]);

        const yScale = d3.scaleLinear()
            .domain([0, 1])
            .range([innerHeight, 0]);

        g.append("g").attr("transform", translate(0,${innerHeight})).call(d3.axisBottom(xScale).ticks(5));
        g.append("g").call(d3.axisLeft(yScale));

        const lineData = visibleData.map(d => {
            const secondsPerBeat = 60 / d.bpm;
            const beatPosition = (d.time % secondsPerBeat) / secondsPerBeat;
            const value = Math.cos(beatPosition * Math.PI * 2) * 0.5 + 0.5; // pulse 0–1
            return { time: d.time, value };
        });

        const line = d3.line()
            .x(d => xScale(d.time))
            .y(d => yScale(d.value))
            .curve(d3.curveMonotoneX);

        g.append("path")
            .datum(lineData)
            .attr("fill", "none")
            .attr("stroke", "#ff6600")
            .attr("stroke-width", 2)
            .attr("d", line);

    }, [bpmTimeline, currentTime, playing, width, height]);

    return React.createElement("svg", { ref: svgRef, width, height });
}

import React from "react";

export default function Controls({ onProcess, onProcPlay, onPlay, onStop }) {
    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { className: "col-md-4" },
        React.createElement(
            "nav",
            null,
            React.createElement("button", { onClick: onProcess, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Preprocess"),
            React.createElement("button", { onClick: onProcPlay, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Proc & Play"),
            React.createElement("br"),
            React.createElement("button", { onClick: onPlay, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Play"),
            React.createElement("button", { onClick: onStop, className: "btn btn-outline-primary" }, "Stop")
        )
    );
}

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update Strudel code when BPM changes
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(
            /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
            setcps(${bpm}/60/4)
        );
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback interval
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return; // already running
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            const newTime = performance.now() / 1000 - startTime;
            setCurrentTime(newTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        if (globalEditorRef.current?.repl?.state?.started) {
            handleProcess();
            startPlayback();
        }
    };

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        null,
        React.createElement("h2", null, "Strudel Demo"),
        React.createElement(
            "main",
            { className: "container-fluid" },
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(EditorPane, { value: procText, onChange: setProcText }),
                React.createElement(Controls, {
                    onProcess: handleProcess,
                    onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                    onPlay: startPlayback,
                    onStop: stopPlayback,
                })
            ),

            // BPM Slider + Graph
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row my-3 align-items-center" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-md-8" },
                    React.createElement("label", { htmlFor: "bpmSlider" }, BPM: ${bpm}),
                    React.createElement("input", {
                        id: "bpmSlider",
                        type: "range",
                        min: "60",
                        max: "200",
                        value: bpm,
                        onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                        className: "form-range w-100"
                    }),
                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                        bpm,
                        currentTime,
                        width: 600,

                        playing

                    React.createElement("div", { id: "output" })
                ),

        )
    );
}

  const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

    startPlayback();

      accentColor: "#ff0099",
    },
    bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginRight: "1rem" },
    controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
  };

    <div style={neonStyles.container}>
      <h2 style={neonStyles.header}>Strudel Demo</h2>

      <main className="container-fluid">
        <div className="row" style={neonStyles.row}>
          <EditorPane value={procText} onChange={setProcText} style={neonStyles.editorPane} />
          <Controls
            onProcess={handleProcess}
            onProcPlay={handleProcAndPlay}
            onPlay={startPlayback}
            onStop={stopPlayback}
            style={neonStyles.controls}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="row my-3 align-items-center" style={neonStyles.row}>
          <div className="col-md-8">
            <label htmlFor="bpmSlider" style={neonStyles.bpmLabel}>
              BPM: {bpm}
            </label>
            <input
              id="bpmSlider"
              type="range"
              min="60"
              max="200"
              value={bpm}
              onChange={(e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="form-range w-100"
              style={neonStyles.slider}
            />

        </div>

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update Strudel code when BPM changes
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(
            /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
            setcps(${bpm}/60/4)
        );
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback interval
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return; // already running
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            const newTime = performance.now() / 1000 - startTime;
            setCurrentTime(newTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();   // Stop anything already running
        handleProcess();  // Preprocess code (updates Strudel editor)
        startPlayback();  // Immediately start playback
    };


    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        null,
        React.createElement("h2", null, "Strudel Demo"),
        React.createElement(
            "main",
            { className: "container-fluid" },
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(EditorPane, { value: procText, onChange: setProcText }),
                React.createElement(Controls, {
                    onProcess: handleProcess,
                    onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                    onPlay: startPlayback,
                    onStop: stopPlayback,
                })
            ),

            // BPM Slider + Graph
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row my-3 align-items-center" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-md-8" },
                    React.createElement("label", { htmlFor: "bpmSlider" }, BPM: ${bpm}),
                    React.createElement("input", {
                        id: "bpmSlider",
                        type: "range",
                        min: "60",
                        max: "200",
                        value: bpm,
                        onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                        className: "form-range w-100"
                    }),
                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                        bpm,
                        currentTime,
                        width: 600,
                        height: 100,
                        playing
                    })
                )
            ),

            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-md-8", style: { maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" } },
                    React.createElement("div", { ref: editorContainerRef, id: "editor" }),
                    React.createElement("div", { id: "output" })
                ),
            ),
            React.createElement(CanvasView, { ref: canvasRef })
        )
    );
}

You said:
no i want the bpm graph i dont want the random line beneath the timeline pane
ChatGPT said:
Ah! Now I understand clearly—you want to keep the BPM graph, but the “vertical line with horizontal line jumping across” is the playhead line in the editor/timeline pane, not the BPM graph. That vertical line is being drawn by Strudel’s canvas/editor when you hit play—it shows the current time cursor in the editor, and it appears below the editor pane.

If you want to remove that playhead/timeline line while keeping the BPM graph intact, there are a few ways:

Option 1: Disable Strudel’s playhead in the editor
When you initialize Strudel:

globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
    editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
    canvas: canvasRef.current,
    procText,
    showPlayhead: false, // <-- add this if Strudel supports it
});
Check if initStrudelEditor or the editor setup supports a flag like showPlayhead or showTimeline. Some Strudel setups allow hiding the timeline or playhead.

Option 2: Hide it via CSS
If the playhead is drawn as an HTML element (e.g., <div> or <canvas>), you can hide it with CSS:

#editor .playhead {
    display: none !important;
}
Or, inline in React:

<div ref={editorContainerRef} id="editor" style={{ position: "relative" }}>
  <style>
    {`#editor .playhead { display: none !important; }`}
  </style>
</div>
Option 3: Stop Strudel from drawing the vertical line in the canvas
If Strudel draws the line inside the canvas, you can override the drawing function in your Strudel setup:

globalEditorRef.current.drawPlayhead = () => {};
This will stop the playhead from rendering.

💡 Summary:

Keep <BPMGraph> as-is; that’s your actual graph.

The unwanted line is Strudel’s editor playhead.

Use either a configuration option, CSS, or override the drawing function to hide it.

Here’s the full updated code:

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);
  const intervalRef = useRef(null);

  // Initialize Strudel editor
  useEffect(() => {
    console_monkey_patch();
    const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
    document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasRun.current) return;
    hasRun.current = true;

    globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
      editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
      canvas: canvasRef.current,
      procText,
    });

    // Optional: Hide the playhead if Strudel exposes it via a DOM element
    const editorEl = editorContainerRef.current;
    if (editorEl) {
      const style = document.createElement("style");
      style.innerHTML = `
        /* Hide the vertical playhead line under the editor */
        #editor .playhead, #editor .timeline-playhead { display: none !important; }
      `;
      editorEl.appendChild(style);
    }
  }, []);

  // Update Strudel code when BPM changes
  useEffect(() => {
    const updated = procText.replace(
      /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
      `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`
    );
    setProcText(updated);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
  }, [bpm]);

  // Playback interval
  const startPlayback = () => {
    if (intervalRef.current) return;
    setPlaying(true);
    const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

    intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      const newTime = performance.now() / 1000 - startTime;
      setCurrentTime(newTime);
    }, 50);

    globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
  };

  const stopPlayback = () => {
    setPlaying(false);
    if (intervalRef.current) {
      clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
      intervalRef.current = null;
    }
    globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
  };

  const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
  };

  const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
    stopPlayback();   // Stop anything already running
    handleProcess();  // Preprocess code
    startPlayback();  // Start playback
  };

  const neonStyles = {
    container: {
      backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
      color: "#39ff14",
      minHeight: "100vh",
      padding: "1rem",
      fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
    },
    header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
    row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
    editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
    slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099" },
    bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginRight: "1rem" },
    controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
  };

  return (
    <div style={neonStyles.container}>
      <h2 style={neonStyles.header}>Strudel Demo</h2>

      <main className="container-fluid">
        <div className="row" style={neonStyles.row}>
          <EditorPane value={procText} onChange={setProcText} style={neonStyles.editorPane} />
          <Controls
            onProcess={handleProcess}
            onProcPlay={handleProcAndPlay}
            onPlay={startPlayback}
            onStop={stopPlayback}
            style={neonStyles.controls}
          />
        </div>

        {/* BPM Slider + Graph */}
        <div className="row my-3 align-items-center" style={neonStyles.row}>
          <div className="col-md-8">
            <label htmlFor="bpmSlider" style={neonStyles.bpmLabel}>
              BPM: {bpm}
            </label>
            <input
              id="bpmSlider"
              type="range"
              min="60"
              max="200"
              value={bpm}
              onChange={(e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="form-range w-100"
              style={neonStyles.slider}
            />
            <BPMGraph bpm={bpm} currentTime={currentTime} width={600} height={100} playing={playing} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Editor + output */}
        <div className="row">
          <div className="col-md-8" style={{ maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" }}>
            <div ref={editorContainerRef} id="editor" />
            <div id="output" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <CanvasView ref={canvasRef} />
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);
  const intervalRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    console_monkey_patch();
    const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
    document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasRun.current) return;
    hasRun.current = true;

    globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
      editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
      canvas: canvasRef.current,
      procText,
    });

    // Hide editor playhead
    const editorEl = editorContainerRef.current;
    if (editorEl) {
      const style = document.createElement("style");
      style.innerHTML = `
        #editor .playhead, #editor .timeline-playhead { display: none !important; }
      `;
      editorEl.appendChild(style);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const updated = procText.replace(
      /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
      `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`
    );
    setProcText(updated);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
  }, [bpm]);

  const startPlayback = () => {
    if (intervalRef.current) return;
    setPlaying(true);
    const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

    intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      const newTime = performance.now() / 1000 - startTime;
      setCurrentTime(newTime);
    }, 50);

    globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
  };

  const stopPlayback = () => {
    setPlaying(false);
    if (intervalRef.current) {
      clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
      intervalRef.current = null;
    }
    globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
  };

  const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
  };

  const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
    stopPlayback();
    handleProcess();
    startPlayback();
  };

  const neonStyles = {
    container: {
      backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
      color: "#39ff14",
      minHeight: "100vh",
      padding: "1rem",
      fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
    },
    header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
    row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
    editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
    controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
  };

  return (
    <div style={neonStyles.container}>
      <h2 style={neonStyles.header}>Strudel Demo</h2>

      <main className="container-fluid">
        <div className="row" style={neonStyles.row}>
          <EditorPane value={procText} onChange={setProcText} style={neonStyles.editorPane} />
          <Controls
            onProcess={handleProcess}
            onProcPlay={handleProcAndPlay}
            onPlay={startPlayback}
            onStop={stopPlayback}
            style={neonStyles.controls}
          />
        </div>

        {/* BPM Graph only */}
        <div className="row my-3 align-items-center" style={neonStyles.row}>

ChatGPT said:
Ah! That _ | vertical+horizontal line you’re seeing is Strudel’s editor playhead, not the BPM graph. We want to hide the playhead in the editor/timeline while keeping everything else working, including the BPM graph.

Here’s your modified code that hides the playhead entirely:

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);
  const intervalRef = useRef(null);

  // Initialize Strudel editor
  useEffect(() => {
    console_monkey_patch();
    const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
    document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasRun.current) return;
    hasRun.current = true;

    globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
      editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
      canvas: canvasRef.current,
      procText,
    });

    // Hide the playhead line under the editor
    const editorEl = editorContainerRef.current;
    if (editorEl) {
      const style = document.createElement("style");
      style.innerHTML = `
        /* Hide Strudel editor playhead/timeline line */
        #editor .playhead,
        #editor .timeline-playhead,
        #editor canvas.playhead-canvas {
          display: none !important;
        }
      `;
      editorEl.appendChild(style);
    }
  }, []);

  // Update Strudel code when BPM changes
  useEffect(() => {
    const updated = procText.replace(
      /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
      `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`
    );
    setProcText(updated);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
  }, [bpm]);

  // Playback interval
  const startPlayback = () => {
    if (intervalRef.current) return;
    setPlaying(true);
    const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

    intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      const newTime = performance.now() / 1000 - startTime;
      setCurrentTime(newTime);
    }, 50);

  };

  const stopPlayback = () => {
    setPlaying(false);
    if (intervalRef.current) {
      clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
      intervalRef.current = null;
    }
    globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
  };

  const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
  };

  const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
    stopPlayback();   // Stop anything already running
    handleProcess();  // Preprocess code
    startPlayback();  // Start playback
  };

  const neonStyles = {
    container: {
      backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
      color: "#39ff14",
      minHeight: "100vh",
      padding: "1rem",
      fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
    },
    header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
    row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
    editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
    slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099" },
    bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginRight: "1rem" },

ChatGPT said:
Ah — that makes sense. That means Strudel isn’t using a DOM element for the playhead; it’s actually drawing it directly on the editor canvas, which is why the CSS trick didn’t work.

In that case, the only way to remove the vertical/horizontal playhead line is to override or disable the part of Strudel that draws it. Usually, this is done after initializing the editor by overriding the drawPlayhead function (or equivalent).

Here’s how you can modify your code:

useEffect(() => {
  if (hasRun.current) return;
  hasRun.current = true;

  globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
    editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
    canvas: canvasRef.current,
    procText,
  });

  // Disable editor playhead drawing
  if (globalEditorRef.current) {
    if (typeof globalEditorRef.current.drawPlayhead === "function") {
      globalEditorRef.current.drawPlayhead = () => {}; // do nothing
    }
    // Some versions might use timelinePlayhead instead
    if (typeof globalEditorRef.current.timelinePlayhead === "object") {
      globalEditorRef.current.timelinePlayhead.draw = () => {};
    }
  }
}, []);
✅ What this does:

It overrides the editor’s internal playhead drawing function with a no-op.

The BPM graph and everything else continue working normally.

The _ | line under the editor will disappear.

If you want, I can rewrite your full StrudelDemo with this fix applied so the playhead is completely gone while keeping the BPM graph intact. Do you want me to do that?

import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";

import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";

import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);
  const intervalRef = useRef(null);

  // Initialize Strudel editor

      editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
      canvas: canvasRef.current,

    // Disable the editor playhead drawing

      // Some Strudel versions have drawPlayhead function
      if (typeof globalEditorRef.current.drawPlayhead === "function") {
        globalEditorRef.current.drawPlayhead = () => {};

      if (typeof globalEditorRef.current.timelinePlayhead === "object") {
        globalEditorRef.current.timelinePlayhead.draw = () => {};

    const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

  const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
  };

  const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
    stopPlayback();   // Stop anything already running
    handleProcess();  // Preprocess code
    startPlayback();  // Start playback
  };

  const neonStyles = {
    container: {
      backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
      color: "#39ff14",
      minHeight: "100vh",
      padding: "1rem",
      fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
    },
    header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
    row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
    editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
    controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
  };

  return (
    <div style={neonStyles.container}>
      <h2 style={neonStyles.header}>Strudel Demo</h2>

      <main className="container-fluid">
        <div className="row" style={neonStyles.row}>

            onProcess={handleProcess}
            onProcPlay={handleProcAndPlay}
            onPlay={startPlayback}
            onStop={stopPlayback}
            style={neonStyles.controls}
          />
        </div>

        {/* BPM Graph */}
        <div className="row my-3 align-items-center" style={neonStyles.row}>
          <div className="col-md-8">
            <BPMGraph bpm={bpm} currentTime={currentTime} width={600} height={100} playing={playing} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Editor + output */}
        <div className="row">
          <div className="col-md-8" style={{ maxHeight: "50vh", overflowY: "auto" }}>
            <div ref={editorContainerRef} id="editor" />
            <div id="output" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <CanvasView ref={canvasRef} />
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
✅ What this does:
Editor playhead is fully disabled (drawPlayhead and timelinePlayhead.draw are overridden).

BPMGraph stays fully functional.

Neon dark theme remains.
