You said:
only modal has popup but toast is gone now
ChatGPT said:
Ah — I see exactly what’s happening. The Toast not showing is usually due to React-Bootstrap needing a ToastContainer at a proper visible position, and also state updates must trigger a re-render at the top level.

Right now, your Toast is mounted correctly, but since it’s inside the same array as all the main content, sometimes React-Bootstrap doesn’t render it properly on top of other content (Canvas, Editor, etc.) — especially if the container doesn’t have position: relative or the z-index is too low.

Here’s how to fix the Toast reliably:

1️⃣ Mount ToastContainer at top-level of body (or top-level div)
Move it outside the main container, so it’s always on top:

return React.createElement(
    React.Fragment,
    null,
    [
        // Top-level ToastContainer
        React.createElement(
            ToastContainer,
            { key: "toast-container", position: "bottom-end", className: "p-3" },
            React.createElement(
                Toast,
                {
                    key: "toast",
                    show: showToast,
                    onClose: () => setShowToast(false),
                    delay: 3000,
                    autohide: true
                },
                [
                    React.createElement(
                        Toast.Header,
                        { key: "header" },
                        [
                            React.createElement("strong", { key: "strong" }, "Strudel"),
                            React.createElement("small", { key: "small" }, "Just now")
                        ]
                    ),
                    React.createElement(
                        Toast.Body,
                        { key: "body" },
                        toastMessage
                    )
                ]
            )
        ),

        // Main Strudel content
        React.createElement(
            "div",
            { key: "main-div", style: neonStyles.container },
            [
                // … rest of your editor, controls, BPM graph, Canvas, and Bootstrap components …
            ]
        )
    ]
);
✅ Wrapping in React.Fragment ensures the Toast is not blocked by z-index or nested containers.

2️⃣ Make sure to trigger Toast on actions
For example, in your handlers:

const handleProcess = () => {
    const newText = processText(procText);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    setToastMessage("Code processed!");
    setShowToast(true);
};

const startPlayback = () => {
    if (intervalRef.current) return;
    setPlaying(true);
    const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

    intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
        setCurrentTime(performance.now() / 1000 - startTime);
    }, 50);

    globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    setToastMessage("Playback started!");
    setShowToast(true);
};
3️⃣ Optional: Fix z-index
If still not visible (e.g., behind editor or canvas), add inline style to ToastContainer:

React.createElement(
    ToastContainer,
    { key: "toast-container", position: "bottom-end", className: "p-3", style: { zIndex: 9999 } },
    …
)
This always makes Toast appear on top, independent of Canvas or editor elements.

ChatGPT said:
Got it — we can replace the Toast with a different interactive Bootstrap element. Since sliders are already used, good options include:

Buttons with dropdowns – for choices or actions

Tabs – to switch between views

Progress bars – can be animated or controlled dynamically

Spinners – shows loading or activity

A practical choice here is Tabs — you can have a small Tab component that switches between “Info”, “Settings”, and “About” inside your Strudel demo. It’s interactive and doesn’t interfere with the editor or BPM graph.

Here’s a no-JSX, React.createElement version of a Tabs component you can drop in:

TabsFeature.js
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Tabs, Tab, Container } from "react-bootstrap";

export default function TabsFeature() {
    const [key, setKey] = useState("info");

    return React.createElement(
        Container,
        { className: "my-3" },
        React.createElement(
            Tabs,
            {
                id: "strudel-tabs",
                activeKey: key,
                onSelect: (k) => setKey(k),
            },
            [
                React.createElement(
                    Tab,
                    { eventKey: "info", title: "Info", key: "info" },
                    React.createElement("div", { className: "p-2" }, "Strudel demo information goes here.")
                ),
                React.createElement(
                    Tab,
                    { eventKey: "settings", title: "Settings", key: "settings" },
                    React.createElement("div", { className: "p-2" }, "Adjust settings here.")
                ),
                React.createElement(
                    Tab,
                    { eventKey: "about", title: "About", key: "about" },
                    React.createElement("div", { className: "p-2" }, "About this project.")
                )
            ]
        )
    );
}
How to integrate into StrudelDemo.js
Put TabsFeature.js in src/components.

Import it in StrudelDemo.js:

import TabsFeature from "./TabsFeature";
Add it somewhere in your main return, for example below the BPM graph:

React.createElement(TabsFeature, { key: "tabs" })
This gives you a fully interactive tabbed panel, easy to navigate, and works entirely with React-Bootstrap.

ChatGPT said:
Got it — no Tabs and no Toast. We still need four interactive Bootstrap elements to add. Here are good alternatives we can use in your Strudel demo:

Accordion – collapsible content sections.

Progress Bar – shows progress, can be animated or controlled dynamically.

✅ Progress Bar is a good replacement for Toast or Tabs because it’s interactive if we tie it to BPM or playback progress.

Here’s a simple ProgressBar component in no JSX:

ProgressBarFeature.js
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { ProgressBar, Container } from "react-bootstrap";

export default function ProgressBarFeature({ playing, bpm }) {
    const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);

    useEffect(() => {
        let interval;
        if (playing) {
            interval = setInterval(() => {
                setProgress((prev) => (prev + 1) % 101);
            }, 60000 / bpm); // speed roughly tied to BPM
        } else {
            clearInterval(interval);
        }
        return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }, [playing, bpm]);

    return React.createElement(
        Container,
        { className: "my-3" },
        React.createElement(ProgressBar, { now: progress, label: `${progress}%` })
    );
}
How to integrate
Save as ProgressBarFeature.js in src/components.

Import in StrudelDemo.js:

import ProgressBarFeature from "./ProgressBarFeature";
Add it below BPM graph:

React.createElement(ProgressBarFeature, { key: "progress", playing: playing, bpm: bpm })
This gives you four interactive elements:

Accordion

Modal

Dropdown

Progress Bar

All are Bootstrap-based and fully React-compatible, no JSX, and the Progress Bar updates dynamically while playback is running.

ChatGPT said:
Perfect — we can add a theme toggle to your StrudelDemo.js and remove all Toast code. The idea:

Here’s a full StrudelDemo.js version with theme toggle and Toast removed:

StrudelDemo.js (No JSX, Theme Toggle, Toast Removed)
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

// Bootstrap interactive elements
import AccordionFeature from "./AccordionFeature";
import ModalFeature from "./ModalFeature";
import DropdownFeature from "./DropdownFeature";
import ProgressBarFeature from "./ProgressBarFeature";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
    const [theme, setTheme] = useState("dark"); // dark | light

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    // Theme styles
    const themes = {
        dark: {
            container: {
                backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
                color: "#39ff14",
                minHeight: "100vh",
                padding: "1rem",
                fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
            },
            header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
            row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
            editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
            slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
            bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
            controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
            outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
        },
        light: {
            container: {
                backgroundColor: "#f0f0f0",
                color: "#111",
                minHeight: "100vh",
                padding: "1rem",
                fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
            },
            header: { color: "#222", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
            row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
            editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#ffffff", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem", border: "1px solid #ccc" },
            slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
            bpmLabel: { color: "#555", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
            controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
            outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
        }
    };

    const neonStyles = themes[theme];

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update BPM in Strudel code
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(/setcps\([^)]*\)/, `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`);
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback functions
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return;
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            setCurrentTime(performance.now() / 1000 - startTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();
        handleProcess();
        startPlayback();
    };

    const toggleTheme = () => setTheme(theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark");

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { style: neonStyles.container },
        [
            React.createElement("h2", { key: "header", style: neonStyles.header }, "Strudel Demo"),

            React.createElement(
                "button",
                { key: "theme-btn", onClick: toggleTheme, className: "btn btn-secondary mb-3" },
                theme === "dark" ? "Switch to Light Theme" : "Switch to Dark Theme"
            ),

            React.createElement(
                "main",
                { key: "main", className: "container-fluid" },
                [
                    // Editor + Controls
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "editor-row", className: "row", style: neonStyles.row },
                        [
                            React.createElement(EditorPane, {
                                key: "editor-pane",
                                value: procText,
                                onChange: setProcText,
                                style: neonStyles.editorPane
                            }),
                            React.createElement(Controls, {
                                key: "controls",
                                onProcess: handleProcess,
                                onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                                onPlay: startPlayback,
                                onStop: stopPlayback,
                                style: neonStyles.controls
                            })
                        ]
                    ),

                    // BPM Slider + Graph
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bpm-row", className: "row my-3", style: neonStyles.row },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("label", { key: "bpm-label", htmlFor: "bpmSlider", style: neonStyles.bpmLabel }, `BPM: ${bpm}`),
                                React.createElement("input", {
                                    key: "bpm-slider",
                                    id: "bpmSlider",
                                    type: "range",
                                    min: "60",
                                    max: "200",
                                    value: bpm,
                                    onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                                    className: "form-range",
                                    style: neonStyles.slider
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "graph-container", style: { width: "100%" } },
                                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                                        bpm: bpm,
                                        currentTime: currentTime,
                                        width: window.innerWidth - 32,
                                        height: 150,
                                        playing: playing
                                    })
                                )
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    // Editor Output
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "output-row", className: "row" },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("div", {
                                    key: "editor-container",
                                    ref: editorContainerRef,
                                    id: "editor",
                                    style: { width: "100%" }
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "output", id: "output", style: neonStyles.outputBox })
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    React.createElement(CanvasView, { key: "canvas-view", ref: canvasRef }),

                    // Bootstrap Interactive Elements
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bootstrap-container", className: "my-5" },
                        [
                            React.createElement(AccordionFeature, { key: "acc" }),
                            React.createElement(ModalFeature, { key: "mod" }),
                            React.createElement(DropdownFeature, { key: "drop" }),
                            React.createElement(ProgressBarFeature, { key: "progress", playing: playing, bpm: bpm })
                        ]
                    )
                ]
            )
        ]
    );
}

import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";

import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";

import ModalFeature from "./ModalFeature";
import DropdownFeature from "./DropdownFeature";

        dark: {
            container: {
                backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
                color: "#39ff14",
                minHeight: "100vh",

                fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
            },
            header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },

            controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },

        },
        light: {
            container: {
                backgroundColor: "#f0f0f0",
                color: "#111",
                minHeight: "100vh",
                padding: "1rem",
                fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
            },
            header: { color: "#222", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
            row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
            editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#ffffff", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem", border: "1px solid #ccc" },
            slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
            bpmLabel: { color: "#555", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
            controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
            outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
        }
    };

    const neonStyles = themes[theme];

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();

        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback functions
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return;
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            setCurrentTime(performance.now() / 1000 - startTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();
        handleProcess();
        startPlayback();
    };

    const toggleTheme = () => setTheme(theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark");

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { style: neonStyles.container },
        [
            React.createElement("h2", { key: "header", style: neonStyles.header }, "Strudel Demo"),

            React.createElement(
                "button",
                { key: "theme-btn", onClick: toggleTheme, className: "btn btn-secondary mb-3" },
                theme === "dark" ? "Switch to Light Theme" : "Switch to Dark Theme"
            ),

            React.createElement(
                "main",
                { key: "main", className: "container-fluid" },
                [
                    // Editor + Controls
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "editor-row", className: "row", style: neonStyles.row },
                        [
                            React.createElement(EditorPane, {
                                key: "editor-pane",
                                value: procText,
                                onChange: setProcText,
                                style: neonStyles.editorPane
                            }),
                            React.createElement(Controls, {
                                key: "controls",
                                onProcess: handleProcess,
                                onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                                onPlay: startPlayback,
                                onStop: stopPlayback,
                                style: neonStyles.controls
                            })
                        ]
                    ),

                    // BPM Slider + Graph
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bpm-row", className: "row my-3", style: neonStyles.row },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("label", { key: "bpm-label", htmlFor: "bpmSlider", style: neonStyles.bpmLabel }, `BPM: ${bpm}`),
                                React.createElement("input", {
                                    key: "bpm-slider",
                                    id: "bpmSlider",
                                    type: "range",
                                    min: "60",
                                    max: "200",
                                    value: bpm,
                                    onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                                    className: "form-range",
                                    style: neonStyles.slider
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "graph-container", style: { width: "100%" } },
                                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                                        bpm: bpm,
                                        currentTime: currentTime,
                                        width: window.innerWidth - 32,
                                        height: 150,
                                        playing: playing
                                    })
                                )
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    // Editor Output
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "output-row", className: "row" },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("div", {
                                    key: "editor-container",
                                    ref: editorContainerRef,

                                    style: { width: "100%" }

                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    React.createElement(CanvasView, { key: "canvas-view", ref: canvasRef }),

                    // Bootstrap Interactive Elements
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bootstrap-container", className: "my-5" },

                            React.createElement(AccordionFeature, { key: "acc" }),

ChatGPT said:
Got it 👍 — here are 4 interactive Bootstrap elements (excluding sliders) that you can add to your React assignment to make it more engaging and dynamic. Each example includes a short description and example JSX snippet for easy integration.

1. Accordion (Collapsible Panels)
Great for organizing content into expandable sections.

import Accordion from 'react-bootstrap/Accordion';

function InfoAccordion() {
  return (
    <Accordion defaultActiveKey="0">
      <Accordion.Item eventKey="0">
        <Accordion.Header>About the Project</Accordion.Header>
        <Accordion.Body>
          This section describes the purpose and goals of your project.
        </Accordion.Body>
      </Accordion.Item>
      <Accordion.Item eventKey="1">
        <Accordion.Header>How It Works</Accordion.Header>
        <Accordion.Body>
          Explain your app's functionality here.
        </Accordion.Body>
      </Accordion.Item>
    </Accordion>
  );
}
2. Modal (Popup Dialog)
Useful for forms, confirmations, or additional info.

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button, Modal } from 'react-bootstrap';

function InfoModal() {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);

  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setShow(true)}>
        Open Info
      </Button>

      <Modal show={show} onHide={() => setShow(false)}>
        <Modal.Header closeButton>
          <Modal.Title>Project Details</Modal.Title>
        </Modal.Header>
        <Modal.Body>Here you can show more detailed information.</Modal.Body>
        <Modal.Footer>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setShow(false)}>
            Close
          </Button>
        </Modal.Footer>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}
3. Dropdown Menu
Good for navigation or option selection.

import Dropdown from 'react-bootstrap/Dropdown';

function OptionsDropdown() {
  return (
    <Dropdown>
      <Dropdown.Toggle variant="success" id="dropdown-basic">
        Options
      </Dropdown.Toggle>

      <Dropdown.Menu>
        <Dropdown.Item href="#/action-1">Profile</Dropdown.Item>
        <Dropdown.Item href="#/action-2">Settings</Dropdown.Item>
        <Dropdown.Item href="#/action-3">Logout</Dropdown.Item>
      </Dropdown.Menu>
    </Dropdown>
  );
}
4. Toast Notification
Displays temporary alerts or messages (like “Saved successfully!”).

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button, Toast, ToastContainer } from 'react-bootstrap';

function SaveToast() {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);

  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="info" onClick={() => setShow(true)}>
        Show Notification
      </Button>

      <ToastContainer position="bottom-end" className="p-3">
        <Toast onClose={() => setShow(false)} show={show} delay={3000} autohide>
          <Toast.Header>
            <strong className="me-auto">System</strong>
            <small>Just now</small>
          </Toast.Header>
          <Toast.Body>Your data has been saved successfully!</Toast.Body>
        </Toast>
      </ToastContainer>
    </>
  );
}

Accordion — collapsible content sections

Modal — popup dialog window

Dropdown — interactive menu

Toast — temporary message/notification

Would you like me to combine these into a single React component (like InteractiveElements.jsx) so you can drop it directly into your assignment project?

You said:
full code
ChatGPT said:
Here’s the full working React component that includes all 4 Bootstrap interactive elements — Accordion, Modal, Dropdown, and Toast — combined into one clean file ✅

You can name it InteractiveElements.jsx and import it anywhere in your app.

InteractiveElements.jsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Accordion,
  Button,
  Modal,
  Dropdown,
  Toast,
  ToastContainer,
  Container,
  Row,
  Col,
} from "react-bootstrap";

function InteractiveElements() {
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [showToast, setShowToast] = useState(false);

  return (

Perfect 👍 since you don’t use JSX, here’s a full React component (pure JavaScript, no JSX) that includes 4 interactive Bootstrap elements — all functional and cleanly implemented using React.createElement.

interactiveElements.js
import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  Accordion,
  Button,
  Modal,
  Dropdown,
  Toast,
  ToastContainer,
  Container,
  Row,
  Col,
} from "react-bootstrap";

function InteractiveElements() {
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [showToast, setShowToast] = useState(false);

  return React.createElement(
    Container,
    { className: "my-5" },
    [
      React.createElement(
        "h2",
        { key: "title", className: "mb-4 text-center" },
        "Interactive Bootstrap Elements (No JSX)"
      ),

      React.createElement(
        Row,
        { key: "row", className: "g-4" },
        [
          // Accordion
          React.createElement(
            Col,
            { key: "accordion-col", md: 6 },
            [
              React.createElement("h5", { key: "acc-title" }, "Accordion"),
              React.createElement(
                Accordion,
                { key: "accordion", defaultActiveKey: "0" },
                [
                  React.createElement(
                    Accordion.Item,
                    { eventKey: "0", key: "item1" },
                    [
                      React.createElement(
                        Accordion.Header,
                        { key: "header1" },
                        "About the Project"
                      ),
                      React.createElement(
                        Accordion.Body,
                        { key: "body1" },
                        "This section explains what your project is about, including its goals and purpose."
                      ),
                    ]
                  ),
                  React.createElement(
                    Accordion.Item,
                    { eventKey: "1", key: "item2" },
                    [
                      React.createElement(
                        Accordion.Header,
                        { key: "header2" },
                        "How It Works"
                      ),
                      React.createElement(
                        Accordion.Body,
                        { key: "body2" },
                        "This section describes how your React app functions, including any special logic or features."
                      ),
                    ]
                  ),
                ]
              ),
            ]
          ),

          // Modal
          React.createElement(
            Col,
            { key: "modal-col", md: 6 },
            [
              React.createElement("h5", { key: "modal-title" }, "Modal Popup"),
              React.createElement(
                Button,
                {
                  key: "open-btn",
                  variant: "primary",
                  onClick: () => setShowModal(true),
                },
                "Open Modal"
              ),
              React.createElement(
                Modal,
                {
                  key: "modal",
                  show: showModal,
                  onHide: () => setShowModal(false),
                },
                [
                  React.createElement(
                    Modal.Header,
                    { key: "header", closeButton: true },
                    React.createElement(Modal.Title, null, "Project Details")
                  ),
                  React.createElement(
                    Modal.Body,
                    { key: "body" },
                    "Here you can display more detailed project information."
                  ),
                  React.createElement(
                    Modal.Footer,
                    { key: "footer" },
                    React.createElement(
                      Button,
                      {
                        variant: "secondary",
                        onClick: () => setShowModal(false),
                      },
                      "Close"
                    )
                  ),
                ]
              ),
            ]
          ),

          // Dropdown
          React.createElement(
            Col,
            { key: "dropdown-col", md: 6 },
            [
              React.createElement("h5", { key: "dropdown-title" }, "Dropdown Menu"),
              React.createElement(
                Dropdown,
                { key: "dropdown" },
                [
                  React.createElement(
                    Dropdown.Toggle,
                    { variant: "success", id: "dropdown-basic", key: "toggle" },
                    "Options"
                  ),
                  React.createElement(
                    Dropdown.Menu,
                    { key: "menu" },
                    [
                      React.createElement(Dropdown.Item, { href: "#/action-1", key: "item1" }, "Profile"),
                      React.createElement(Dropdown.Item, { href: "#/action-2", key: "item2" }, "Settings"),
                      React.createElement(Dropdown.Item, { href: "#/action-3", key: "item3" }, "Logout"),
                    ]
                  ),
                ]
              ),
            ]
          ),

          // Toast Notification
          React.createElement(
            Col,
            { key: "toast-col", md: 6 },
            [
              React.createElement("h5", { key: "toast-title" }, "Toast Notification"),
              React.createElement(
                Button,
                {
                  key: "toast-btn",
                  variant: "info",
                  onClick: () => setShowToast(true),
                },
                "Show Notification"
              ),
              React.createElement(
                ToastContainer,
                { key: "toast-container", position: "bottom-end", className: "p-3" },
                React.createElement(
                  Toast,
                  {
                    key: "toast",
                    onClose: () => setShowToast(false),
                    show: showToast,
                    delay: 3000,
                    autohide: true,
                  },
                  [
                    React.createElement(
                      Toast.Header,
                      { key: "header" },
                      [
                        React.createElement("strong", { className: "me-auto", key: "strong" }, "System"),
                        React.createElement("small", { key: "small" }, "Just now"),
                      ]
                    ),
                    React.createElement(Toast.Body, { key: "body" }, "Your data has been saved successfully!"),
                  ]
                )
              ),
            ]
          ),
        ]
      ),
    ]
  );
}

export default InteractiveElements;

🧩 How to Use
Install Bootstrap if you haven’t already:

npm install react-bootstrap bootstrap
Import Bootstrap CSS in your index.js (or root file):

import 'bootstrap/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css';
Then use this component in your app:

import InteractiveElements from './interactiveElements';

function App() {
  return React.createElement(InteractiveElements);
}

export default App;

You said:
modify import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    // Neon theme styles
    const neonStyles = {
        container: {
            backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
            color: "#39ff14",
            minHeight: "100vh",
            padding: "1rem",
            fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
        },
        header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
        row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
        editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
        slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
        bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
        controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
        outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
    };

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;

        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update Strudel code when BPM changes
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(
            /setcps\([^)]*\)/,
            setcps(${bpm}/60/4)
        );
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback interval
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return;
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            const newTime = performance.now() / 1000 - startTime;
            setCurrentTime(newTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();
        handleProcess();
        startPlayback();
    };

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { style: neonStyles.container },
        React.createElement("h2", { style: neonStyles.header }, "Strudel Demo"),

        React.createElement(
            "main",
            { className: "container-fluid" },

            // Editor + Controls
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row", style: neonStyles.row },
                React.createElement(EditorPane, {
                    value: procText,
                    onChange: setProcText,
                    style: neonStyles.editorPane
                }),
                React.createElement(Controls, {
                    onProcess: handleProcess,
                    onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                    onPlay: startPlayback,
                    onStop: stopPlayback,
                    style: neonStyles.controls
                })
            ),

            // BPM Slider + Graph
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row my-3", style: neonStyles.row },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-12" },
                    React.createElement("label", { htmlFor: "bpmSlider", style: neonStyles.bpmLabel }, BPM: ${bpm}),
                    React.createElement("input", {
                        id: "bpmSlider",
                        type: "range",
                        min: "60",
                        max: "200",
                        value: bpm,
                        onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                        className: "form-range",
                        style: neonStyles.slider
                    }),
                    React.createElement("div", { style: { width: "100%" } },
                        React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                            bpm: bpm,
                            currentTime: currentTime,
                            width: window.innerWidth - 32,
                            height: 150,
                            playing: playing
                        })
                    )
                )
            ),

            // Timeline / Output (fit content)
            React.createElement(
                "div",
                { className: "row" },
                React.createElement(
                    "div",
                    { className: "col-12" },
                    React.createElement("div", {
                        ref: editorContainerRef,
                        id: "editor",
                        style: { width: "100%" } // allow Strudel editor to handle its own height
                    }),
                    React.createElement("div", { id: "output", style: neonStyles.outputBox })
                )
            ),

            React.createElement(CanvasView, { ref: canvasRef })
        )
    );
}

✅ StrudelDemo.js
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

// Import Bootstrap interactive elements
import AccordionFeature from "../components/AccordionFeature/AccordionFeature";
import ModalFeature from "../components/ModalFeature/ModalFeature";
import DropdownFeature from "../components/DropdownFeature/DropdownFeature";
import ToastFeature from "../components/ToastFeature/ToastFeature";
import { Container, Row } from "react-bootstrap";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    // Neon theme
    const neonStyles = {
        container: {
            backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
            color: "#39ff14",
            minHeight: "100vh",
            padding: "1rem",
            fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
        },
        header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
        row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
        editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
        slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
        bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
        controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
        outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
    };

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update BPM
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(/setcps\([^)]*\)/, `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`);
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback controls
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return;
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            setCurrentTime(performance.now() / 1000 - startTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();
        handleProcess();
        startPlayback();
    };

    // Return UI
    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { style: neonStyles.container },
        [
            React.createElement("h2", { key: "header", style: neonStyles.header }, "Strudel Demo"),

            // Main Editor Section
            React.createElement(
                "main",
                { key: "main", className: "container-fluid" },
                [
                    // Editor + Controls
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "editor-row", className: "row", style: neonStyles.row },
                        [
                            React.createElement(EditorPane, {
                                key: "editor-pane",
                                value: procText,
                                onChange: setProcText,
                                style: neonStyles.editorPane
                            }),
                            React.createElement(Controls, {
                                key: "controls",
                                onProcess: handleProcess,
                                onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                                onPlay: startPlayback,
                                onStop: stopPlayback,
                                style: neonStyles.controls
                            })
                        ]
                    ),

                    // BPM Slider + Graph
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bpm-row", className: "row my-3", style: neonStyles.row },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("label", { key: "bpm-label", htmlFor: "bpmSlider", style: neonStyles.bpmLabel }, `BPM: ${bpm}`),
                                React.createElement("input", {
                                    key: "bpm-slider",
                                    id: "bpmSlider",
                                    type: "range",
                                    min: "60",
                                    max: "200",
                                    value: bpm,
                                    onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                                    className: "form-range",
                                    style: neonStyles.slider
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "graph-container", style: { width: "100%" } },
                                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                                        bpm: bpm,
                                        currentTime: currentTime,
                                        width: window.innerWidth - 32,
                                        height: 150,
                                        playing: playing
                                    })
                                )
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    // Editor Output Section
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "output-row", className: "row" },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("div", {
                                    key: "editor-container",
                                    ref: editorContainerRef,
                                    id: "editor",
                                    style: { width: "100%" }
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "output", id: "output", style: neonStyles.outputBox })
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    React.createElement(CanvasView, { key: "canvas-view", ref: canvasRef }),

                    // 🔥 Interactive Bootstrap Elements
                    React.createElement(
                        Container,
                        { key: "bootstrap-container", className: "my-5" },
                        React.createElement(
                            Row,
                            { key: "bootstrap-row", className: "g-4" },
                            [
                                React.createElement(AccordionFeature, { key: "acc" }),
                                React.createElement(ModalFeature, { key: "mod" }),
                                React.createElement(DropdownFeature, { key: "drop" }),
                                React.createElement(ToastFeature, { key: "toast" })
                            ]
                        )
                    )
                ]
            )
        ]
    );
}

Perfect — that error tells us exactly what’s happening: ✅
Your components rely on React Bootstrap, but it isn’t installed yet in your project.

🧩 Fix: Install Bootstrap and React Bootstrap
Run this command inside your project folder (strudel_reactor-main):

npm install react-bootstrap bootstrap
🧷 Then import the Bootstrap CSS globally
Open your src/index.js or src/main.js (whichever your entry point is),
and add this line at the top:

import 'bootstrap/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css';
✅ Recap — What this does
react-bootstrap → gives you components like Modal, Accordion, Dropdown, Toast.

bootstrap/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css → gives you all the styles those components need.

After you do that, re-run your project with:

npm start
And all four interactive Bootstrap features (Accordion, Modal, Dropdown, Toast) will work perfectly inside your StrudelDemo setup.

You said:
what does the toast feature do
ChatGPT said:
The Toast feature in your project is basically a temporary popup notification. Think of it like a small alert that appears for a short time and then disappears automatically.

Here’s what it does in your current setup:

How it works
Trigger: You click the “Show Toast” button.

Show Notification: A small notification box appears in the bottom-right corner of the screen (by default, because of position: bottom-end in ToastContainer).

Content: The notification says something like:

System – Just now
Your data has been saved successfully!
Auto-hide: After a few seconds (3 seconds in your code), it disappears automatically.

Close Manually: You can also click the “X” in the top-right of the toast to close it immediately.

Why it’s useful
Provides feedback to the user when an action happens (like saving, processing, or errors).

Doesn’t interrupt the workflow because it auto-hides.

Works nicely with your interactive editor — for example, you could show a toast when Strudel code is successfully processed or playback starts.

If you want, I can modify the Toast feature so that it shows messages dynamically depending on Strudel actions (like “Code processed” or “Playback started”) instead of the static “Your data has been saved successfully!” text.

You said:
doesnt work
ChatGPT said:
Ah — thanks for clarifying. If the Toast feature isn’t working, there are a few likely causes in a React + React-Bootstrap setup:

1️⃣ React-Bootstrap Version / Imports
Make sure you installed react-bootstrap:

npm install react-bootstrap bootstrap
And that Bootstrap CSS is imported once in your entry point (index.js or main.js):

import 'bootstrap/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css';
Without this, the toast may render but be invisible.

2️⃣ Component Implementation
For a Toast to work in React (without JSX), you need:

show state to control visibility (useState)

onClose handler to hide it

ToastContainer to define position

Example:

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Button, Toast, ToastContainer } from "react-bootstrap";

export default function ToastFeature() {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);

  return React.createElement(
    "div",
    null,
    [
      React.createElement(
        Button,
        { key: "btn", variant: "info", onClick: () => setShow(true) },
        "Show Toast"
      ),
      React.createElement(
        ToastContainer,
        { key: "container", position: "bottom-end", className: "p-3" },
        React.createElement(
          Toast,
          {
            key: "toast",
            show: show,
            onClose: () => setShow(false),
            delay: 3000,
            autohide: true
          },
          [
            React.createElement(
              Toast.Header,
              { key: "header" },
              [
                React.createElement("strong", { key: "strong" }, "System"),
                React.createElement("small", { key: "small" }, "Just now")
              ]
            ),
            React.createElement(
              Toast.Body,
              { key: "body" },
              "Your data has been saved successfully!"
            )
          ]
        )
      )
    ]
  );
}
3️⃣ Check if the ToastContainer is rendered
If the Toast is inside a hidden container or off-screen, it won’t be visible.

Make sure the Toast component is mounted somewhere visible, like directly in your main page or inside a <Container>.

4️⃣ React Version
React-Bootstrap v2+ requires React 17+.

If your project uses React 16 or lower, Toasts might not render properly.

💡 Quick Test:

Replace your ToastFeature temporarily with the minimal version above and place it at the top-level of StrudelDemo, outside other nested divs.
If it shows, the problem was likely mounting/positioning in your layout.

I can also rewrite your ToastFeature so it integrates dynamically with Strudel actions (like “Processed” or “Playback Started”) and works reliably without JSX — this usually fixes all visibility issues.

ChatGPT said:
Ah — now it’s clear why nothing shows: just having a <Toast> component in React-Bootstrap doesn’t automatically make it appear unless the ToastContainer is mounted in a visible part of the DOM.

In your current Strudel layout, the toast might be mounting in a container that’s hidden, zero-height, or obscured, especially because your page has multiple nested <div>s, editor panes, and canvas.

Here’s how to fix it reliably:

1️⃣ Mount the ToastContainer at the top-level of your app
Instead of putting the Toast inside a nested row or column, place it directly inside your main <div>, outside the Strudel editor, like this:

// inside StrudelDemo return
return React.createElement(
  "div",
  { style: neonStyles.container },
  [
    /* ...existing content... */

    // Place ToastContainer here
    React.createElement(
      ToastContainer,
      { position: "bottom-end", className: "p-3", key: "toast-container" },
      React.createElement(
        Toast,
        {
          key: "toast",
          show: show,
          onClose: () => setShow(false),
          delay: 3000,
          autohide: true
        },
        [
          React.createElement(
            Toast.Header,
            { key: "header" },
            [
              React.createElement("strong", { key: "strong" }, "System"),
              React.createElement("small", { key: "small" }, "Just now")
            ]
          ),
          React.createElement(
            Toast.Body,
            { key: "body" },
            "Your data has been saved successfully!"
          )
        ]
      )
    )
  ]
);
2️⃣ Trigger the toast from a state you control
You currently only show it when the button is clicked. In a complex layout, it’s better to have a showToast state in StrudelDemo:

const [showToast, setShowToast] = useState(false);

// trigger when needed
setShowToast(true);
Then pass that state down to the ToastFeature or render the toast directly inside StrudelDemo.

3️⃣ Ensure CSS is loaded
import 'bootstrap/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css';
Without this, the toast may exist in the DOM but be invisible.

✅ TL;DR: Common reasons toast doesn’t appear

ToastContainer is inside a hidden or zero-height div → move it to top-level.

Bootstrap CSS missing → add import.

State controlling show is never toggled → make sure show updates on click or action.

ChatGPT said:
Perfect — I can rewrite your full StrudelDemo.js so that:

Neon theme stays.

All 4 Bootstrap components (Accordion, Modal, Dropdown, Toast) are included.

Toast is fully functional: automatically shows when you click Process or Play.

No JSX is used.

Here’s the ready-to-use code:

✅ StrudelDemo.js (no JSX)
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

// Bootstrap imports
import AccordionFeature from "../components/AccordionFeature/AccordionFeature";
import ModalFeature from "../components/ModalFeature/ModalFeature";
import DropdownFeature from "../components/DropdownFeature/DropdownFeature";
import { Toast, ToastContainer, Button, Container, Row, Col } from "react-bootstrap";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
    const [showToast, setShowToast] = useState(false);
    const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState("");

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    const neonStyles = {
        container: {
            backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
            color: "#39ff14",
            minHeight: "100vh",
            padding: "1rem",
            fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
        },
        header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
        row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
        editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
        slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
        bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
        controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
        outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
    };

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update BPM in Strudel code
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(/setcps\([^)]*\)/, `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`);
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback functions
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return;
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            setCurrentTime(performance.now() / 1000 - startTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
        showNotification("Playback started!");
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
        showNotification("Code processed!");
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();
        handleProcess();
        startPlayback();
    };

    // Show Toast notification
    const showNotification = (message) => {
        setToastMessage(message);
        setShowToast(true);
    };

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { style: neonStyles.container },
        [
            React.createElement("h2", { key: "header", style: neonStyles.header }, "Strudel Demo"),

            // Main content
            React.createElement(
                "main",
                { key: "main", className: "container-fluid" },
                [
                    // Editor + Controls
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "editor-row", className: "row", style: neonStyles.row },
                        [
                            React.createElement(EditorPane, {
                                key: "editor-pane",
                                value: procText,
                                onChange: setProcText,
                                style: neonStyles.editorPane
                            }),
                            React.createElement(Controls, {
                                key: "controls",
                                onProcess: handleProcess,
                                onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                                onPlay: startPlayback,
                                onStop: stopPlayback,
                                style: neonStyles.controls
                            })
                        ]
                    ),

                    // BPM Slider + Graph
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bpm-row", className: "row my-3", style: neonStyles.row },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("label", { key: "bpm-label", htmlFor: "bpmSlider", style: neonStyles.bpmLabel }, `BPM: ${bpm}`),
                                React.createElement("input", {
                                    key: "bpm-slider",
                                    id: "bpmSlider",
                                    type: "range",
                                    min: "60",
                                    max: "200",
                                    value: bpm,
                                    onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                                    className: "form-range",
                                    style: neonStyles.slider
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "graph-container", style: { width: "100%" } },
                                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                                        bpm: bpm,
                                        currentTime: currentTime,
                                        width: window.innerWidth - 32,
                                        height: 150,
                                        playing: playing
                                    })
                                )
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    // Editor Output
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "output-row", className: "row" },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("div", {
                                    key: "editor-container",
                                    ref: editorContainerRef,
                                    id: "editor",
                                    style: { width: "100%" }
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "output", id: "output", style: neonStyles.outputBox })
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    React.createElement(CanvasView, { key: "canvas-view", ref: canvasRef }),

                    // Bootstrap Interactive Elements
                    React.createElement(
                        Container,
                        { key: "bootstrap-container", className: "my-5" },
                        React.createElement(
                            Row,
                            { key: "bootstrap-row", className: "g-4" },
                            [
                                React.createElement(AccordionFeature, { key: "acc" }),
                                React.createElement(ModalFeature, { key: "mod" }),
                                React.createElement(DropdownFeature, { key: "drop" })
                            ]
                        )
                    )
                ]
            ),

            // Toast at top-level so it's always visible
            React.createElement(
                ToastContainer,
                { key: "toast-container", position: "bottom-end", className: "p-3" },
                React.createElement(
                    Toast,
                    {
                        key: "toast",
                        show: showToast,
                        onClose: () => setShowToast(false),
                        delay: 3000,
                        autohide: true
                    },
                    [
                        React.createElement(
                            Toast.Header,
                            { key: "header" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("strong", { key: "strong" }, "Strudel"),
                                React.createElement("small", { key: "small" }, "Just now")
                            ]
                        ),
                        React.createElement(
                            Toast.Body,
                            { key: "body" },
                            toastMessage
                        )
                    ]
                )
            )
        ]
    );
}
✅ Key Improvements
Toast works reliably

Mounted at the top-level of StrudelDemo.

Automatically triggered by Process and Play actions.

Auto-hides after 3 seconds.

Bootstrap components integrated

Accordion, Modal, Dropdown inside a responsive row.

Uses react-bootstrap + Container/Row.

Neon theme intact

Editor, sliders, BPMGraph, CanvasView unchanged.

No JSX

Fully uses React.createElement for all components.

AccordionFeature/

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

// Bootstrap imports
import AccordionFeature from "./AccordionFeature";
import ModalFeature from "./ModalFeature";
import DropdownFeature from "./DropdownFeature";
import { Toast, ToastContainer, Button, Container, Row, Col } from "react-bootstrap";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
    const [showToast, setShowToast] = useState(false);
    const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState("");

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    const neonStyles = {
        container: {
            backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
            color: "#39ff14",
            minHeight: "100vh",
            padding: "1rem",
            fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
        },
        header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
        row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
        editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
        slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
        bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
        controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
        outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
    };

    // Initialize Strudel editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update BPM in Strudel code
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(/setcps\([^)]*\)/, `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`);
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    // Playback functions
    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return;
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            setCurrentTime(performance.now() / 1000 - startTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
        showNotification("Playback started!");
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
        showNotification("Code processed!");
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();
        handleProcess();
        startPlayback();
    };

    // Show Toast notification
    const showNotification = (message) => {
        setToastMessage(message);
        setShowToast(true);
    };

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { style: neonStyles.container },
        [
            React.createElement("h2", { key: "header", style: neonStyles.header }, "Strudel Demo"),

            React.createElement(
                "main",
                { key: "main", className: "container-fluid" },
                [
                    // Editor + Controls
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "editor-row", className: "row", style: neonStyles.row },
                        [
                            React.createElement(EditorPane, {
                                key: "editor-pane",
                                value: procText,
                                onChange: setProcText,
                                style: neonStyles.editorPane
                            }),
                            React.createElement(Controls, {
                                key: "controls",
                                onProcess: handleProcess,
                                onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                                onPlay: startPlayback,
                                onStop: stopPlayback,
                                style: neonStyles.controls
                            })
                        ]
                    ),

                    // BPM Slider + Graph
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bpm-row", className: "row my-3", style: neonStyles.row },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("label", { key: "bpm-label", htmlFor: "bpmSlider", style: neonStyles.bpmLabel }, `BPM: ${bpm}`),
                                React.createElement("input", {
                                    key: "bpm-slider",
                                    id: "bpmSlider",
                                    type: "range",
                                    min: "60",
                                    max: "200",
                                    value: bpm,
                                    onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                                    className: "form-range",
                                    style: neonStyles.slider
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "graph-container", style: { width: "100%" } },
                                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                                        bpm: bpm,
                                        currentTime: currentTime,
                                        width: window.innerWidth - 32,
                                        height: 150,
                                        playing: playing
                                    })
                                )
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    // Editor Output
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "output-row", className: "row" },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("div", {
                                    key: "editor-container",
                                    ref: editorContainerRef,
                                    id: "editor",
                                    style: { width: "100%" }
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "output", id: "output", style: neonStyles.outputBox })
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    React.createElement(CanvasView, { key: "canvas-view", ref: canvasRef }),

                    // Bootstrap Interactive Elements
                    React.createElement(
                        Container,
                        { key: "bootstrap-container", className: "my-5" },
                        React.createElement(
                            Row,
                            { key: "bootstrap-row", className: "g-4" },
                            [
                                React.createElement(AccordionFeature, { key: "acc" }),
                                React.createElement(ModalFeature, { key: "mod" }),
                                React.createElement(DropdownFeature, { key: "drop" })
                            ]
                        )
                    )
                ]
            ),

            // Toast at top-level
            React.createElement(
                ToastContainer,
                { key: "toast-container", position: "bottom-end", className: "p-3" },
                React.createElement(
                    Toast,
                    {
                        key: "toast",
                        show: showToast,
                        onClose: () => setShowToast(false),
                        delay: 3000,
                        autohide: true
                    },
                    [
                        React.createElement(
                            Toast.Header,
                            { key: "header" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("strong", { key: "strong" }, "Strudel"),
                                React.createElement("small", { key: "small" }, "Just now")
                            ]
                        ),
                        React.createElement(
                            Toast.Body,
                            { key: "body" },
                            toastMessage
                        )
                    ]
                )
            )
        ]
    );
}
