ChatGPT said:
Absolutely! Here’s the full StrudelDemo.js with the save presets, dropdown integration, and “X” remove button fully implemented.

Everything is drop-in ready.

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { processText } from "../utils/textProcessor";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import Controls from "./Controls";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";

// Bootstrap interactive elements
import AccordionFeature from "./AccordionFeature";
import ModalFeature from "./ModalFeature";
import DropdownFeature from "./DropdownFeature";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
    const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
    const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
    const [theme, setTheme] = useState("dark");
    const [savedPresets, setSavedPresets] = useState([]);

    const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
    const canvasRef = useRef(null);
    const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
    const hasRun = useRef(false);
    const intervalRef = useRef(null);

    const themes = {
        dark: {
            container: {
                backgroundColor: "#0f0f1a",
                color: "#39ff14",
                minHeight: "100vh",
                padding: "1rem",
                fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
            },
            header: { color: "#00fff7", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
            row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
            editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" },
            slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
            bpmLabel: { color: "#ff77ff", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
            controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
            outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
        },
        light: {
            container: {
                backgroundColor: "#f0f0f0",
                color: "#111",
                minHeight: "100vh",
                padding: "1rem",
                fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace",
            },
            header: { color: "#222", textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem" },
            row: { marginBottom: "1rem" },
            editorPane: { backgroundColor: "#ffffff", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem", border: "1px solid #ccc" },
            slider: { accentColor: "#ff0099", width: "100%" },
            bpmLabel: { color: "#555", marginBottom: "0.5rem", display: "block" },
            controls: { marginTop: "0.5rem" },
            outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
        }
    };

    const neonStyles = themes[theme];

    // Load saved presets and last config
    useEffect(() => {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem("strudelConfigList");
        if (saved) setSavedPresets(JSON.parse(saved));

        const last = localStorage.getItem("strudelConfig");
        if (last) {
            const cfg = JSON.parse(last);
            if (cfg.bpm) setBpm(cfg.bpm);
            if (cfg.procText) setProcText(cfg.procText);
        }
    }, []);

    // Initialize Strudel Editor
    useEffect(() => {
        console_monkey_patch();
        const handleD3Data = (event) => console.log(event.detail);
        document.addEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
        return () => document.removeEventListener("d3Data", handleD3Data);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasRun.current) return;
        hasRun.current = true;
        globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
            editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
            canvas: canvasRef.current,
            procText,
        });
    }, []);

    // Update BPM in Strudel code
    useEffect(() => {
        const updated = procText.replace(/setcps\([^)]*\)/, `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`);
        setProcText(updated);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
    }, [bpm]);

    const startPlayback = () => {
        if (intervalRef.current) return;
        setPlaying(true);
        const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;

        intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
            setCurrentTime(performance.now() / 1000 - startTime);
        }, 50);

        globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
    };

    const stopPlayback = () => {
        setPlaying(false);
        if (intervalRef.current) {
            clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
            intervalRef.current = null;
        }
        globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
    };

    const handleProcess = () => {
        const newText = processText(procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(newText);
    };

    const handleProcAndPlay = () => {
        stopPlayback();
        handleProcess();
        startPlayback();
    };

    const toggleTheme = () => setTheme(theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark");

    // Save preset
    const handleSaveConfig = () => {
        const config = { bpm, procText, name: `Preset ${savedPresets.length + 1}` };
        const newList = [...savedPresets, config];
        setSavedPresets(newList);
        localStorage.setItem("strudelConfigList", JSON.stringify(newList));
        localStorage.setItem("strudelConfig", JSON.stringify(config));
        console.log("Saved configuration:", config);
    };

    const handleSelectPreset = (index) => {
        const preset = savedPresets[index];
        if (!preset) return;
        setBpm(preset.bpm);
        setProcText(preset.procText);
        globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(preset.procText);
        console.log("Loaded preset:", preset.name);
    };

    const handleRemovePreset = (index) => {
        const newList = savedPresets.filter((_, i) => i !== index);
        setSavedPresets(newList);
        localStorage.setItem("strudelConfigList", JSON.stringify(newList));
    };

    return React.createElement(
        "div",
        { style: neonStyles.container },
        [
            React.createElement("h2", { key: "header", style: neonStyles.header }, "Strudel Demo"),

            React.createElement(
                "button",
                { key: "theme-btn", onClick: toggleTheme, className: "btn btn-secondary mb-3" },
                theme === "dark" ? "Switch to Light Theme" : "Switch to Dark Theme"
            ),

            React.createElement(
                "main",
                { key: "main", className: "container-fluid" },
                [
                    // Editor + Controls
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "editor-row", className: "row", style: neonStyles.row },
                        [
                            React.createElement(EditorPane, {
                                key: "editor-pane",
                                value: procText,
                                onChange: setProcText,
                                style: neonStyles.editorPane
                            }),
                            React.createElement(Controls, {
                                key: "controls",
                                onProcess: handleProcess,
                                onProcPlay: handleProcAndPlay,
                                onPlay: startPlayback,
                                onStop: stopPlayback,
                                style: neonStyles.controls
                            })
                        ]
                    ),

                    // BPM Slider + Graph + Save
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bpm-row", className: "row my-3", style: neonStyles.row },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("label", { key: "bpm-label", htmlFor: "bpmSlider", style: neonStyles.bpmLabel }, `BPM: ${bpm}`),
                                React.createElement("input", {
                                    key: "bpm-slider",
                                    id: "bpmSlider",
                                    type: "range",
                                    min: "60",
                                    max: "200",
                                    value: bpm,
                                    onChange: (e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value)),
                                    className: "form-range",
                                    style: neonStyles.slider
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "graph-container", style: { width: "100%" } },
                                    React.createElement(BPMGraph, {
                                        bpm: bpm,
                                        currentTime: currentTime,
                                        width: window.innerWidth - 32,
                                        height: 150,
                                        playing: playing
                                    })
                                ),
                                React.createElement(
                                    "button",
                                    { key: "save-btn", onClick: handleSaveConfig, className: "btn btn-success mt-3" },
                                    "Save Configuration"
                                )
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    // Editor Output
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "output-row", className: "row" },
                        React.createElement(
                            "div",
                            { className: "col-12" },
                            [
                                React.createElement("div", {
                                    key: "editor-container",
                                    ref: editorContainerRef,
                                    id: "editor",
                                    style: { width: "100%" }
                                }),
                                React.createElement("div", { key: "output", id: "output", style: neonStyles.outputBox })
                            ]
                        )
                    ),

                    React.createElement(CanvasView, { key: "canvas-view", ref: canvasRef }),

                    // Interactive Elements
                    React.createElement(
                        "div",
                        { key: "bootstrap-container", className: "my-5", style: { display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", gap: "16px" } },
                        [
                            // Centered Accordion
                            React.createElement(
                                "div",
                                { key: "acc-wrapper", style: { width: "100%", display: "flex", justifyContent: "center" } },
                                React.createElement(AccordionFeature, { key: "acc" })
                            ),

                            // DropdownFeature with saved presets
                            React.createElement(DropdownFeature, {
                                key: "drop",
                                savedPresets: savedPresets,
                                onSelectPreset: handleSelectPreset,
                                onRemovePreset: handleRemovePreset
                            }),

                            // ModalFeature
                            React.createElement(ModalFeature, { key: "mod" })
                        ]
                    )
                ]
            )
        ]
    );
}
✅ Features Implemented
Save current BPM + editor text as a preset.

Dropdown dynamically shows all saved presets.

Each preset has an “X” button to remove it.

Clicking a preset loads it instantly (BPM + editor).

1. StrudelDemo.js
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import console_monkey_patch from "../utils/console-monkey-patch";
import { initStrudelEditor } from "../utils/strudelSetup";
import { stranger_tune } from "../tunes";
import CanvasView from "./CanvasView";
import EditorPane from "./EditorPane";
import BPMGraph from "./BPMGraph";
import ControlsPanel from "./ControlsPanel";
import AccordionFeature from "./AccordionFeature";
import ModalFeature from "./ModalFeature";
import DropdownFeature from "./DropdownFeature";

export default function StrudelDemo() {
  const [procText, setProcText] = useState(stranger_tune);
  const [bpm, setBpm] = useState(140);
  const [speed, setSpeed] = useState(1.0);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState("dark");
  const [savedPresets, setSavedPresets] = useState([]);

  const editorContainerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const globalEditorRef = useRef(null);
  const hasRun = useRef(false);
  const intervalRef = useRef(null);

  const neonStyles = {
    container: { padding: "1rem", minHeight: "100vh", fontFamily: "'Courier New', Courier, monospace", backgroundColor: theme === "dark" ? "#0f0f1a" : "#f0f0f0", color: theme === "dark" ? "#39ff14" : "#111" },
    header: { textAlign: "center", marginBottom: "1rem", color: theme === "dark" ? "#00fff7" : "#222" },
    outputBox: { textAlign: "left", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }
  };

  // Load saved presets
  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("strudelConfigList");
    if (saved) setSavedPresets(JSON.parse(saved));

    const last = localStorage.getItem("strudelConfig");
    if (last) {
      const cfg = JSON.parse(last);
      if (cfg.bpm) setBpm(cfg.bpm);
      if (cfg.procText) setProcText(cfg.procText);
      if (cfg.speed) setSpeed(cfg.speed || 1.0);
    }
  }, []);

  // Initialize Strudel editor
  useEffect(() => {
    console_monkey_patch();
    if (hasRun.current) return;
    hasRun.current = true;

    globalEditorRef.current = initStrudelEditor({
      editorContainer: editorContainerRef.current,
      canvas: canvasRef.current,
      procText,
    });
  }, []);

  // Update BPM in editor
  useEffect(() => {
    const updated = procText.replace(/setcps\([^)]*\)/, `setcps(${bpm}/60/4)`);
    setProcText(updated);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(updated);
  }, [bpm]);

  const startPlayback = () => {
    if (intervalRef.current) return;
    setPlaying(true);
    const startTime = performance.now() / 1000 - currentTime;
    intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setCurrentTime((performance.now() / 1000 - startTime) * speed);
    }, 50);
    globalEditorRef.current?.evaluate();
  };

  const stopPlayback = () => {
    setPlaying(false);
    if (intervalRef.current) {
      clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
      intervalRef.current = null;
    }
    globalEditorRef.current?.stop();
  };

  const handleSaveConfig = () => {
    const config = { bpm, speed, procText, name: `Preset ${savedPresets.length + 1}` };
    const newList = [...savedPresets, config];
    setSavedPresets(newList);
    localStorage.setItem("strudelConfigList", JSON.stringify(newList));
    localStorage.setItem("strudelConfig", JSON.stringify(config));
  };

  const handleSelectPreset = (index) => {
    const preset = savedPresets[index];
    if (!preset) return;
    setBpm(preset.bpm);
    setSpeed(preset.speed || 1.0);
    setProcText(preset.procText);
    globalEditorRef.current?.setCode(preset.procText);
  };

  const handleRemovePreset = (index) => {
    const newList = savedPresets.filter((_, i) => i !== index);
    setSavedPresets(newList);
    localStorage.setItem("strudelConfigList", JSON.stringify(newList));
  };

  const toggleTheme = () => setTheme(theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark");

  return (
    <div style={neonStyles.container}>
      <h2 style={neonStyles.header}>Strudel Demo</h2>
      <button className="btn btn-secondary mb-3" onClick={toggleTheme}>
        {theme === "dark" ? "Switch to Light Theme" : "Switch to Dark Theme"}
      </button>

      <div className="container-fluid">
        <div className="row mb-3">
          <EditorPane value={procText} onChange={setProcText} style={{ backgroundColor: theme === "dark" ? "#1a1a2e" : "#fff", borderRadius: "8px", padding: "0.5rem" }} />
          <ControlsPanel
            bpm={bpm}
            setBpm={setBpm}
            speed={speed}
            setSpeed={setSpeed}
            onSave={handleSaveConfig}
            onStart={startPlayback}
            onStop={stopPlayback}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="row my-3">
          <div className="col-12">
            <BPMGraph bpm={bpm} currentTime={currentTime} width={window.innerWidth - 32} height={150} playing={playing} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="row">
          <div className="col-12" ref={editorContainerRef}></div>
          <div style={neonStyles.outputBox}></div>
        </div>

        <CanvasView ref={canvasRef} />

        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", gap: "16px", marginTop: "2rem" }}>
          <div style={{ width: "100%", display: "flex", justifyContent: "center" }}>
            <AccordionFeature />
          </div>

          <DropdownFeature
            savedPresets={savedPresets}
            onSelectPreset={handleSelectPreset}
            onRemovePreset={handleRemovePreset}
          />

          <ModalFeature />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
2. ControlsPanel.js (BPM + Speed + Play/Stop + Save)
import React from "react";

export default function ControlsPanel({ bpm, setBpm, speed, setSpeed, onSave, onStart, onStop }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ marginLeft: "1rem" }}>
      <label>BPM: {bpm}</label>
      <input
        type="range"
        min="60"
        max="200"
        value={bpm}
        onChange={(e) => setBpm(Number(e.target.value))}
        className="form-range"
      />

      <label>Speed: {speed.toFixed(2)}x</label>
      <input
        type="range"
        min="0.1"
        max="3"
        step="0.05"
        value={speed}
        onChange={(e) => setSpeed(Number(e.target.value))}
        className="form-range"
      />

      <div style={{ marginTop: "0.5rem" }}>
        <button className="btn btn-primary me-2" onClick={onStart}>Play</button>
        <button className="btn btn-secondary me-2" onClick={onStop}>Stop</button>
        <button className="btn btn-success" onClick={onSave}>Save Configuration</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
This setup:

BPM slider controls beats per minute.

Speed slider multiplies playback speed (currentTime calculation).

Play / Stop / Save are managed centrally in ControlsPanel.

Presets are saved in DropdownFeature with “X” to remove.

Everything is modular and easy to extend.

I can also update BPMGraph so it scales properly with speed if you want it to visually follow the playback rate.

Part B Marking Rubric (Total Points 100)
 UI & Controls (35 points)
 React is a UI focused language, and your design is being evaluated based on its visuals. Look, Feel, UI,
 and UX are important in this assignment. Your design should look cohesive and be visually appealing. 
You could accomplish this with, for example, cohesively styled Bootstrap components, style sheets, 
component animations, as well as being responsive to user input.
 You also need to create a number of Bootstrap controls / components to interact and audibly change the
 song being played. At least 5 different types of control are expected, and you can see some of the 
available ones on the Bootstrap website: https://getbootstrap.com/docs/5.3/components/. If you want to 
use something else other than Bootstrap, just let me know (usually it’s fine).
Some example Bootstrap components you could use in your project (not an exhaustive list):
 Buttons to save and load settings.
 Radio Buttons to enable specific combinations of instruments.
 Check Boxes to enable specific effects.
 Sliders to control volume, reverb, or other effects.
 Text or number entry to control song speed.
 Hotkeys to apply specific combinations of controls.
 Accordion to allow hiding or expanding different groups of controls.
 Alerts if a problem or validation error occurs.

or type of controls. E.g. only Buttons. Control selection leaves much to be desired (e.g. mostly on/off 
switches).

component types met, although some controls are very similar, or their purpose is unclear.

Components and layout are sensible, although may still require some iteration before being sufficiently 
cohesive.
 D: Design of website thoughtful and stylish. Styling is consistent and high quality. Components and 
layout are intuitive, and all areas of the site are complete.
 HD: Exceptionally high quality UI and UX. Clear a large amount of thought and effort went into 
optimising look and feel of website.
 Preprocessing Logic, General Functionality, and Website Maintainability (35 points)
 In addition to looking visually appealing, your website needs to be functional and maintainable. Your 
website should be bug free, and gracefully handle errors. Your text preprocessing logic should fire 
reliably, and handle any combination of control inputs. You should have a single parent component 
handling state, and managing the flow of data between other components. Your code should also be 
maintainable. Variable and Function names should be descriptive. Code should be organised, 
commented, and thoughtfully designed. 
F2: No effort.

Javascript functions everywhere.

but not well thought out. Data flow between components inconsistent. Writing largely Javascript 
instead of using available React features. Some code comments.
 C: Reasonable refactoring and organisation of code. All major areas are separated into logical 
components. Component hierarchy may not be fully thought through and still require iteration. Using 
React features, although frequently mixed with plain Javascript logic. Data flow between components 
present, but a bit janky, or relying on hard-coded values. Uses code comments appropriately.
 D: Code refactoring and organisation good. Code includes useful comments. Thinking in React instead 
of Javascript. Data flow between components similarly well designed and consistent. Website is bug 
free, and handles improper user input correctly. Common React gotchas like loading components twice 
avoided.
 HD: Perfect. Code extremely well organised. All areas of website use modular components, and 
component hierarchy well thought out. Logic routing and data flow clear and well documented.

 Additional features (15 points)
 In addition to the main requirements above, a few extra features from our class activities are also 
required. The implementation of these is more flexible, and up to you, although a suggested activity for
 each is available.
 JSON Handling: 
You must be able to read and write JSON data in some manner. If you’re unsure how to incorporate 
this, I suggest you either implement a save and load state feature for your settings, or convert the 
current state of your controls to a JSON object that can be used as an input for your preprocessor logic.
 A D3 Graph: 
You must produce a D3 graph. I’ve included some starter code that builds and exposes an Array from 
your Strudel 
 .log() methods. See this video https://lo.unisa.edu.au/mod/resource/view.php?
 id=4144147 for what this data looks like. This Array is limited to 100 values, and is updated in real 
time, so your D3 graph also needs to also needs to update with the Array.
 F2: Missing or features completely broken.
 F1: Some effort, but features clearly incomplete or buggy. 
P2 – P1: One or both features attempted, but with some problems or oversights. E.g. JSON handling 
feature attempted, but usage or impact is dubious / trivial, or D3 graph attempted, but errors in output 
like broken axes or cropped text.
 C: JSON handling feature complete. JSON data is able to be read and written for some useful purpose, 
like storing state of options, or saving and loading settings or presents.
 D: JSON handling feature complete plus D3 graph feature attempted, but D3 feature may be 
incomplete or missing aspects.
HD: Both JSON handling and D3 graph features are fully complete and integrated into website.
 Video (10 points)
 You need to include a video (screen recording with facecam) of you demonstrating your website and 
features. I’d like a short (~5 mins) “live performance” of your selected / created song, using all the 
features and preprocessing components you’ve created.
 F2: No video.
 F1: Video or audio was rubbish quality (e.g you recorded your screen via shaky phone). No face-cam 
in the screen capture. Didn’t meaningfully use any of the controls created (e.g. just clicked play).
 P2 – P1: Video quality was passable. Some controls used. Demonstration wasn’t well rehearsed, 
disorganised, or was not very engaging.
 C: Video and audio quality were good. Face cam. Most controls used. Demonstration seemed rehearsed
 and was enjoyable. Control usage clearly changed audible aspects of the music.
 D: Excellent video and audio quality. All controls used. Demonstration was fun and engaging. 
Attention paid to progression and sequence of controls use to audibly impact aspects of song.
 HD: Demonstration went hard.  All controls used. Controls were used to affect the song in particularly 
interesting and enjoyable ways.
 GIGA HD: Up to 10 bonus points are available for exceptionally high production-quality or otherwise 
interesting videos (video editing, effects, etc.). If your live demo goes crazy, or there are bodies on the 
dance floor, or you have pro quality production, and you want to claim these points, please describe 
what you did in your README.md file.
 Music (0 points)
 This isn’t a music theory or composition class, so there’s no points for you to lose for not being a 
musician or musically inclined.
 However, you must submit your assignment with a song that actually plays and your React based 
controls must audibly impact your song. You can reuse and remix songs you find on the Strudel.cc 
bakery. If you do this, please attribute them clearly in your README.md file. And, for the sake of the 
markers, please don’t make your song actively unpleasant to listen to. You do not have to create your 
own song.
 GIGA HD: Up to 20 bonus points are available for original beats that go particularly hard. If you want 
to claim these points, please describe your creative and technical process for creating your song in the 
README.md file. Your documentation needs to be sufficient that I’m confidant you didn’t just 
copy/paste a good song from somewhere else.
README.md file (5 points)
 Having a good README file is important to communicate to the marker how your website works, as 
well as the key features of your project.
 In plain text please describe:
 What each of your controls do.
 Any quirks or usage guidelines that markers may need to know about.
 A link to your demonstration video.
 Any bonus points you’d like to claim, as well as required evidence (See the GIGA HD guidelines in the
 Music and Video sections).
 Any song code you’ve used from the Strudel.cc bakery (or any other source).
 What AI tools you used, and your inputs and outputs (See AI usage guidelines).
 Git History (0)
 You must use Git in this assignment. While there’s no marks available for good Git usage, significant 
penalties exist for poor Git usage. I need to be confidant you’ve worked on this assignment regularly, 
not left everything to the last minute. The way you demonstrate that is by having a reasonable Git 
commit history. Thankfully, making Git commits is really easy.
 If you’re using Visual Studio, and the Git window is not
 active, go to the Git menu and select the “Commit or
 Stash” option. 
Then:
 1. Name your commit based on what you did
 2. Commit the change.
 If you’re using a different editor, I trust you already know what you’re doing. Just remember, when in 
doubt, 
 git add ., 
 git commit -m “your message here”.
Late Submissions
 Late Submissions are permitted, with a 10% penalty for each calendar day late your Assignment is. The
 10% penalty is a flat deduction per day, up to a maximum of 5 days late. E.g., If you submitted your 
Assignment 12 hours late, and would have received a grade of 70%, you would instead receive a grade 
of 60%.
 AI Usage Guidelines
 React is a language with a lot of boilerplate code, as well as a large amount of LLM training data. I 
generally consider it a reasonable target for AI generated code. I’ve tried to craft this assignment in 
such a way that AI is likely to cause you more problems than it solves, and LLM’s should be less able 
to trivialise the assignment. Steps I’ve taken to hinder AI tools include: using a (relatively) obscure 
framework that has less AI training data; working with audio; a requirement to integrate and use many 
features at once; a focus on subjective cohesion and quality of your UI and Code. As such, I don’t mind
 you using AI to assist you with writing code for this assignment. However, if you’d like to use AI 
generated code, you must document every instance of your input prompts and output of the AI 
generated code that makes it into your assignment in your README.md file. Documented AI slop will 
still attract a suitably poor grade for your assignment. You must still be able to explain your code and 
justify design decisions during your Week 13 checkpoint, regardless of your AI documentation.
 To make things explicit, for this assignment, properly documented usage of AI generated code is 
permitted (input prompts and AI output must both be documented in your README.md). 
Undocumented usage of AI generated code is still considered an academic integrity violation. 
Uploading your Project
 While you will be primarily be working from Github, the Uni still needs a copy of your assignments. In
 order to submit your Git repo locally, follow these steps.
 1. Close Visual Studio (or other IDE) and navigate to the folder containing your Node.js web 
application.
 2. Zip up the entire web application. This folder must include your project directory and files plus 
you Git directory as pictured. 
If you’re on Mac, hidden folders like “.git” might be excluded from your archive by default! 
3. Remove the node_modules folder from the Zip file before submission, as it seriously bloats the
 file size. If you remove the node_modules before zipping, you’ll have to npm install to get your 
project dependencies reinstalled.
 4. Upload to the course website. There’s an additional “text response” area, where you can include
 your Github link.
 Good luck in your project!
 I hope this project will improve your React and general webdev skills, while also allowing you to work 
on a ‘fully realised’ project that’s also interesting and unique. I’m hoping this is the type of assignment 
hopefully be an assignment you can add to your portfolio that’s not just another boring React to-do list.
